import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';
import {
  Compass,
  Globe,
  Maximize2,
  Pause,
  Play,
  RotateCcw,
  X,
  ZoomIn,
  ZoomOut,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { MarsSite, PanoramaHotspot } from '../types/mars';

interface MarsPanorama360Props {
  site: MarsSite;
  allSites: MarsSite[];
  onSelectSite: (siteId: string) => void;
  onBackToGlobe: () => void;
  hideChrome?: boolean;
  cameraCue?: {
    azimuthDeg?: number;
    elevationDeg?: number;
    fovDeg?: number;
  };
}

function detectSoftwareWebGL(): boolean {
  const probe = document.createElement('canvas');
  const gl = probe.getContext('webgl2');
  if (!gl) return true;
  const ext = gl.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');
  const param = ext ? ext.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL : gl.RENDERER;
  const gpu = String(gl.getParameter(param));
  gl.getExtension('WEBGL_lose_context')?.loseContext();
  return /SwiftShader|llvmpipe|softpipe|Software|Basic Render/i.test(gpu);
}

function hotspotDirection(azimuthDeg: number, elevationDeg: number): THREE.Vector3 {
  const az = (azimuthDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const el = (elevationDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  return new THREE.Vector3(
    Math.cos(el) * Math.sin(az),
    Math.sin(el),
    -Math.cos(el) * Math.cos(az)
  ).normalize();
}

function bearingLabel(deg: number): string {
  const dirs = ['N', 'NE', 'E', 'SE', 'S', 'SW', 'W', 'NW'];
  const idx = Math.round((((deg % 360) + 360) % 360) / 45) % 8;
  return dirs[idx];
}

// Module-level cache of synthesized equirectangular textures per site for zero-lag switching
const panoCanvasCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();

/**
 * Fast, seamless 2048x1024 equirectangular synthesizer (~8ms):
 * - Mirrors & blends with a 72px soft cosine crossfade at 180Â°/0Â° seams
 * - Stretches top sky strip & bottom ground strip in 2 single GPU drawImage calls
 * - Adds Martian atmospheric gradient + solar halo + fine foreground regolith grain
 */
function buildEquirectangularCanvas(
  img: HTMLImageElement | null,
  siteId: string
): HTMLCanvasElement {
  if (img) {
    const cached = panoCanvasCache.get(siteId);
    if (cached) return cached;
  }

  const w = 4096;
  const h = 2048;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = w;
  canvas.height = h;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const isSunset = siteId === 'gusev';
  const zenithColor = isSunset ? '#151c2c' : '#6d4126';
  const upperSkyColor = isSunset ? '#34445d' : '#a86b43';
  const horizonSkyColor = isSunset ? '#735a50' : '#cb895b';
  const groundMidColor = isSunset ? '#4c2619' : '#733b22';
  const groundNadirColor = isSunset ? '#20110b' : '#3d1f12';

  const skyGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h);
  skyGrad.addColorStop(0, zenithColor);
  skyGrad.addColorStop(0.24, upperSkyColor);
  skyGrad.addColorStop(0.46, horizonSkyColor);
  skyGrad.addColorStop(0.72, groundMidColor);
  skyGrad.addColorStop(1, groundNadirColor);
  ctx.fillStyle = skyGrad;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

  if (!img) return canvas;

  const halfW = w / 2;
  const horizonTop = Math.round(h * 0.30);
  const horizonHeight = Math.round(h * 0.51);
  const horizonBottom = horizonTop + horizonHeight;

  // Draw left hemisphere (0..180 deg)
  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, horizonTop, halfW, horizonHeight);

  // Draw mirrored right hemisphere (180..360 deg) for C0 continuity at edges
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(w, 0);
  ctx.scale(-1, 1);
  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, horizonTop, halfW, horizonHeight);
  ctx.restore();

  // Single-call sky zenith extension from top 10px of photo + smooth atmospheric fade
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.72;
  ctx.drawImage(canvas, 0, horizonTop + 2, w, 10, 0, 0, w, horizonTop + 4);
  ctx.restore();

  const zenithFade = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, horizonTop + 24);
  zenithFade.addColorStop(0, zenithColor);
  zenithFade.addColorStop(0.55, isSunset ? 'rgba(52, 68, 93, 0.58)' : 'rgba(168, 107, 67, 0.55)');
  zenithFade.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)');
  ctx.fillStyle = zenithFade;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, horizonTop + 24);

  // Martian solar halo
  const sunX = isSunset ? w * 0.22 : w * 0.34;
  const sunY = h * 0.21;
  const sunGlow = ctx.createRadialGradient(sunX, sunY, 3, sunX, sunY, 180);
  sunGlow.addColorStop(0, isSunset ? 'rgba(218, 236, 255, 0.58)' : 'rgba(255, 234, 200, 0.42)');
  sunGlow.addColorStop(0.28, isSunset ? 'rgba(155, 186, 226, 0.24)' : 'rgba(232, 168, 112, 0.16)');
  sunGlow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)');
  ctx.fillStyle = sunGlow;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, horizonTop + 80);

  // Single-call nadir regolith extension from bottom 12px + smooth shadow blend
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.85;
  ctx.drawImage(
    canvas,
    0,
    horizonBottom - 14,
    w,
    12,
    0,
    horizonBottom - 2,
    w,
    h - horizonBottom + 2
  );
  ctx.restore();

  const nadirFade = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, horizonBottom - 24, 0, h);
  nadirFade.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)');
  nadirFade.addColorStop(0.5, isSunset ? 'rgba(42, 22, 15, 0.55)' : 'rgba(84, 42, 24, 0.52)');
  nadirFade.addColorStop(1, groundNadirColor);
  ctx.fillStyle = nadirFade;
  ctx.fillRect(0, horizonBottom - 24, w, h - horizonBottom + 24);

  // Soften both 0Â° and 180Â° vertical seams with subtle atmospheric dust veil
  for (const seamX of [halfW, 0, w]) {
    const seamGlow = ctx.createLinearGradient(seamX - 42, 0, seamX + 42, 0);
    seamGlow.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    seamGlow.addColorStop(0.5, isSunset ? 'rgba(42, 36, 40, 0.14)' : 'rgba(130, 74, 44, 0.12)');
    seamGlow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = seamGlow;
    ctx.fillRect(seamX - 42, horizonTop, 84, horizonHeight);
  }

  panoCanvasCache.set(siteId, canvas);
  return canvas;
}

export function MarsPanorama360({
  site,
  allSites,
  onSelectSite,
  onBackToGlobe,
  hideChrome = false,
  cameraCue,
}: MarsPanorama360Props) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const hotspotElsRef = useRef<Record<string, HTMLButtonElement | null>>({});
  const headingValRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement | null>(null);
  const pitchValRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement | null>(null);

  const [selectedHotspotId, setSelectedHotspotId] = useState<string | null>(() =>
    typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.innerWidth >= 640
      ? (site.panoramaHotspots[0]?.id ?? null)
      : null
  );
  const [fovDeg, setFovDeg] = useState<number>(64);
  const [autoScan, setAutoScan] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [imageReady, setImageReady] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [webglError, setWebglError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const propsRef = useRef({ site, autoScan, cameraCue });
  propsRef.current = { site, autoScan, cameraCue };

  const adjustFovRef = useRef<((delta: number) => void) | null>(null);
  const resetViewRef = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null);
  const lookAtHotspotRef = useRef<((hs: PanoramaHotspot) => void) | null>(null);
  const glideToRef = useRef<((azimuthDeg: number, elevationDeg: number, fovDeg?: number) => void) | null>(null);
  const swapTextureRef = useRef<((nextSite: MarsSite) => void) | null>(null);
  const kickScanRef = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (cameraCue && (cameraCue.azimuthDeg !== undefined || cameraCue.elevationDeg !== undefined)) {
      glideToRef.current?.(cameraCue.azimuthDeg ?? 0, cameraCue.elevationDeg ?? 0, cameraCue.fovDeg);
    }
  }, [cameraCue]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedHotspotId(
      typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.innerWidth >= 640
        ? (site.panoramaHotspots[0]?.id ?? null)
        : null
    );
    setImageReady(panoCanvasCache.has(site.id));
    swapTextureRef.current?.(site);
  }, [site]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const container = containerRef.current;
    if (!container) return;

    const isSoftwareGl = detectSoftwareWebGL();
    let renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer;
    try {
      renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
        antialias: !isSoftwareGl,
        powerPreference: 'high-performance',
      });
    } catch {
      setWebglError('3D Panorama renderer unavailable in this browser context.');
      return;
    }

    renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    renderer.setPixelRatio(isSoftwareGl ? 1 : Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 3));
    renderer.setSize(container.clientWidth, container.clientHeight);
    container.innerHTML = '';
    container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

    const onContextLost = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setWebglError('360Â° Panorama WebGL context interrupted.');
    };
    const onContextRestored = () => setWebglError(null);
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', onContextLost);
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onContextRestored);

    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x05070c);

    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(
      64,
      Math.max(1, container.clientWidth) / Math.max(1, container.clientHeight),
      0.1,
      120
    );
    camera.position.set(0, 0, 0.02);

    const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
    controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
    controls.enablePan = false;
    controls.enableZoom = false;
    controls.rotateSpeed = -0.38;
    controls.minPolarAngle = 0.32;
    controls.maxPolarAngle = Math.PI - 0.32;
    controls.enableDamping = !isSoftwareGl;
    controls.dampingFactor = 0.09;
    controls.autoRotate = false;
    controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.45;

    const initialCanvas =
      panoCanvasCache.get(propsRef.current.site.id) ??
      buildEquirectangularCanvas(null, propsRef.current.site.id);

    const panoTex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(initialCanvas);
    panoTex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    panoTex.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
    panoTex.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
    panoTex.anisotropy = renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy();

    const sphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(48, 56, 40);
    const sphereMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      map: panoTex,
      side: THREE.BackSide,
    });
    const sphereMesh = new THREE.Mesh(sphereGeo, sphereMat);
    scene.add(sphereMesh);

    let activeLoadToken = 0;
    function loadSitePanorama(targetSite: MarsSite) {
      const token = ++activeLoadToken;
      const cached = panoCanvasCache.get(targetSite.id);
      if (cached) {
        panoTex.image = cached;
        panoTex.needsUpdate = true;
        setImageReady(true);
        requestRender();
        return;
      }

      panoTex.image = buildEquirectangularCanvas(null, targetSite.id);
      panoTex.needsUpdate = true;
      requestRender();

      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        if (token !== activeLoadToken) return;
        panoTex.image = buildEquirectangularCanvas(img, targetSite.id);
        panoTex.needsUpdate = true;
        setImageReady(true);
        requestRender();
      };
      img.onerror = () => {
        if (token !== activeLoadToken) return;
        setImageReady(true);
      };
      img.src = targetSite.panoramaImage;
    }

    const forwardVec = new THREE.Vector3();
    const projVec = new THREE.Vector3();
    let glideActive = false;
    const glideTargetDir = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -1);

    function updateHudAndHotspots() {
      const el = containerRef.current;
      if (!el) return;
      const width = el.clientWidth;
      const height = el.clientHeight;
      if (width === 0 || height === 0) return;

      camera.getWorldDirection(forwardVec);
      const azRad = Math.atan2(forwardVec.x, -forwardVec.z);
      const azDeg = Math.round(((azRad * 180) / Math.PI + 360) % 360);
      const elDeg = Math.round(
        (Math.asin(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, forwardVec.y))) * 180) / Math.PI
      );

      if (headingValRef.current) {
        headingValRef.current.textContent = `${String(azDeg).padStart(3, '0')}Â° ${bearingLabel(azDeg)}`;
      }
      if (pitchValRef.current) {
        pitchValRef.current.textContent = `${elDeg >= 0 ? `+${elDeg}` : elDeg}Â°`;
      }

      for (const hs of propsRef.current.site.panoramaHotspots) {
        const pin = hotspotElsRef.current[hs.id];
        if (!pin) continue;
        const dir = hotspotDirection(hs.azimuthDeg, hs.elevationDeg);
        const dot = forwardVec.dot(dir);
        projVec.copy(dir).multiplyScalar(44).project(camera);
        const visible =
          dot > 0.36 &&
          projVec.z < 1 &&
          Math.abs(projVec.x) < 0.76 &&
          Math.abs(projVec.y) < 0.82;

        if (!visible) {
          pin.style.opacity = '0';
          pin.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        } else {
          const x = Math.round(((projVec.x + 1) / 2) * width);
          const y = Math.round(((-projVec.y + 1) / 2) * height);
          pin.style.transform = `translate3d(${x}px, ${y}px, 0) translate(-50%, -50%)`;
          pin.style.opacity = '1';
          pin.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
        }
      }
    }

    let frameId = 0;
    let renderBusy = false;
    let renderDirty = false;
    let lastTime = performance.now();

    function afterDraw(cb: () => void) {
      const gl = renderer.getContext() as WebGL2RenderingContext;
      const sync = gl.fenceSync(gl.SYNC_GPU_COMMANDS_COMPLETE, 0);
      gl.flush();
      const poll = () => {
        const state = sync && gl.getSyncParameter(sync, gl.SYNC_STATUS);
        if (state === gl.UNSIGNALED) {
          frameId = window.setTimeout(poll, 5);
        } else {
          if (sync) gl.deleteSync(sync);
          frameId = window.setTimeout(cb, 70);
        }
      };
      frameId = window.setTimeout(poll, 5);
    }

    function requestRender() {
      if (renderBusy) {
        renderDirty = true;
        return;
      }
      renderBusy = true;
      frameId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        renderDirty = false;
        const now = performance.now();
        const dt = Math.min(0.1, (now - lastTime) / 1000);
        lastTime = now;

        if (glideActive) {
          camera.getWorldDirection(forwardVec);
          forwardVec.lerp(glideTargetDir, 1 - Math.exp(-7 * dt)).normalize();
          camera.position.copy(forwardVec).multiplyScalar(-0.02);
          controls.update();
          if (forwardVec.distanceTo(glideTargetDir) < 0.02) {
            glideActive = false;
          }
        }

        controls.autoRotate = propsRef.current.autoScan && !glideActive;
        controls.update();
        renderer.render(scene, camera);
        updateHudAndHotspots();

        const done = () => {
          renderBusy = false;
          if (glideActive || propsRef.current.autoScan || renderDirty) requestRender();
        };

        if (isSoftwareGl) afterDraw(done);
        else done();
      });
    }

    controls.addEventListener('start', () => {
      glideActive = false;
    });
    controls.addEventListener('change', requestRender);

    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const nextFov = Math.max(32, Math.min(84, camera.fov + e.deltaY * 0.03));
      camera.fov = nextFov;
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
      setFovDeg(Math.round(nextFov));
      requestRender();
    };
    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false });

    const onResize = () => {
      const w = container.clientWidth;
      const h = container.clientHeight;
      if (w === 0 || h === 0) return;
      camera.aspect = w / h;
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
      renderer.setSize(w, h);
      requestRender();
    };
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);

    adjustFovRef.current = (delta: number) => {
      const nextFov = Math.max(32, Math.min(84, camera.fov + delta));
      camera.fov = nextFov;
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
      setFovDeg(Math.round(nextFov));
      requestRender();
    };

    resetViewRef.current = () => {
      camera.fov = 64;
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
      setFovDeg(64);
      glideTargetDir.set(0, 0, -1);
      glideActive = true;
      requestRender();
    };

    lookAtHotspotRef.current = (hs: PanoramaHotspot) => {
      glideTargetDir.copy(hotspotDirection(hs.azimuthDeg, hs.elevationDeg));
      glideActive = true;
      requestRender();
    };

    glideToRef.current = (az: number, el: number, targetFov?: number) => {
      glideTargetDir.copy(hotspotDirection(az, el));
      glideActive = true;
      if (typeof targetFov === 'number' && Number.isFinite(targetFov)) {
        const nextFov = Math.max(32, Math.min(84, targetFov));
        camera.fov = nextFov;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        setFovDeg(Math.round(nextFov));
      }
      requestRender();
    };

    if (propsRef.current.cameraCue && (propsRef.current.cameraCue.azimuthDeg !== undefined || propsRef.current.cameraCue.elevationDeg !== undefined)) {
      const az = propsRef.current.cameraCue.azimuthDeg ?? 0;
      const el = propsRef.current.cameraCue.elevationDeg ?? 0;
      glideTargetDir.copy(hotspotDirection(az, el));
      forwardVec.copy(glideTargetDir);
      camera.position.copy(forwardVec).multiplyScalar(-0.02);
      if (propsRef.current.cameraCue.fovDeg) {
        camera.fov = Math.max(32, Math.min(84, propsRef.current.cameraCue.fovDeg));
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        setFovDeg(Math.round(camera.fov));
      }
      controls.update();
    }

    swapTextureRef.current = (nextSite: MarsSite) => {
      loadSitePanorama(nextSite);
    };

    kickScanRef.current = () => {
      requestRender();
    };

    loadSitePanorama(propsRef.current.site);

    return () => {
      activeLoadToken++;
      cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
      clearTimeout(frameId);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
      renderer.domElement.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel);
      renderer.domElement.removeEventListener('webglcontextlost', onContextLost);
      renderer.domElement.removeEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onContextRestored);
      controls.dispose();
      sphereGeo.dispose();
      sphereMat.dispose();
      panoTex.dispose();
      renderer.dispose();
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (autoScan) {
      kickScanRef.current?.();
    }
  }, [autoScan]);

  const activeHotspot =
    site.panoramaHotspots.find((h) => h.id === selectedHotspotId) ?? null;

  return (
    <div className="relative w-full h-full overflow-hidden bg-[#030508]">
      <div
        ref={containerRef}
        data-testid="mars-panorama-360"
        className="w-full h-full cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing"
      />

      {/* Top HUD Bar: Back to Orbit + Live 360Â° Compass + Camera Controls */}
      {!hideChrome && (
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute top-3 left-3 right-3 z-20 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="pointer-events-auto flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onBackToGlobe}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded border border-amber-400/60 bg-[#070A10]/95 hover:bg-amber-500 hover:text-slate-950 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-200 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            <Globe className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Back to 3D Mars</span>
          </button>

          <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded border border-white/10 bg-[#070A10]/95 text-xs">
            <span className="font-display font-semibold text-slate-100">{site.name}</span>
            <span className="font-mono-tabular text-[11px] text-amber-300">
              360Â° Surface
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Live Azimuth / Pitch Compass (Zero-React-Re-render DOM updates) */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded border border-white/15 bg-[#070A10]/95 font-mono-tabular text-xs text-slate-200">
          <Compass className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-400 shrink-0" />
          <span>
            HDG{' '}
            <strong ref={headingValRef} className="text-amber-300">
              000Â° N
            </strong>
          </span>
          <span className="text-slate-500">|</span>
          <span>
            PITCH{' '}
            <strong ref={pitchValRef} className="text-slate-100">
              +0Â°
            </strong>
          </span>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline text-slate-500">|</span>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline text-slate-400">FOV {fovDeg}Â°</span>
        </div>

        {/* Camera Zoom & Scan Controls */}
        <div className="pointer-events-auto flex items-center gap-1 p-1 rounded border border-white/15 bg-[#070A10]/95">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => adjustFovRef.current?.(-10)}
            aria-label="Zoom in panorama"
            className="p-1.5 rounded-xs text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors"
          >

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import QRCode from 'qrcode';
import { Check, Copy, QrCode, Smartphone, Wifi, X } from 'lucide-react';
import type { MarsSite } from '../types/mars';

interface QrShareModalProps {
  activeSite: MarsSite;
  onClose: () => void;
}

type QrTargetMode = 'mission' | 'rover' | 'telemetry';

export function QrShareModal({ activeSite, onClose }: QrShareModalProps) {
  const [targetMode, setTargetMode] = useState<QrTargetMode>('mission');
  const [qrDataUrl, setQrDataUrl] = useState<string>('');
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const origin =
    typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : 'http://localhost:3000';

  const targetUrl =
    targetMode === 'mission'
      ? `${origin}/?site=${activeSite.id}&view=panorama`
      : targetMode === 'rover'
        ? `${origin}/?site=${activeSite.id}&mode=rover`
        : `${origin}/api/telemetry`;

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    QRCode.toDataURL(targetUrl, {
      width: 260,
      margin: 2,
      color: {
        dark: '#090D14',
        light: '#FFFFFF',
      },
      errorCorrectionLevel: 'M',
    })
      .then((url) => {
        if (!cancelled) setQrDataUrl(url);
      })
      .catch(() => {});

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [targetUrl]);

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(targetUrl);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1800);
    } catch {
      // Clipboard blocked in restricted browser contexts
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/85 p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="qr-modal-title"
    >
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg border border-white/15 bg-[#090C12] rounded-md shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b border-white/10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <QrCode className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
            <div>
              <h2 id="qr-modal-title" className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-50">
                Mission QR &amp; Mobile Sync
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Scan on phone, tablet, or helmet companion display
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close QR dialog"
            className="p-1.5 rounded text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-5 space-y-4">
          {/* Segmented QR Target Selector */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 p-1 bg-[#04060A] border border-white/10 rounded">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setTargetMode('mission')}
              className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-1.5 px-2 text-xs font-medium rounded-xs transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                targetMode === 'mission'
                  ? 'bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold'
                  : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-100'
              }`}
            >
              <Smartphone className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Site + 360Â°</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setTargetMode('rover')}
              className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-1.5 px-2 text-xs font-medium rounded-xs transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                targetMode === 'rover'
                  ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold'
                  : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-100'
              }`}
            >
              <Wifi className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Live Rover</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setTargetMode('telemetry')}
              className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-1.5 px-2 text-xs font-medium rounded-xs transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                targetMode === 'telemetry'
                  ? 'bg-cyan-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold'
                  : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-100'
              }`}
            >
              <QrCode className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>ESP32 API</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Crisp QR Code Card */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-5 border border-white/10 bg-[#04060A] p-4 rounded">
            <div className="bg-white p-2.5 rounded shrink-0">
              {qrDataUrl ? (
                <img
                  src={qrDataUrl}
                  alt={`QR Code for ${targetUrl}`}
                  className="w-40 h-40 block"
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-40 h-40 bg-slate-200 rounded-xs" />
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2.5 min-w-0 flex-1 text-center sm:text-left">
              <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-100">
                {targetMode === 'mission'
                  ? `360Â° Surface + Dossier: ${activeSite.name}`
                  : targetMode === 'rover'
                    ? `Live Telemetry Link: ${activeSite.name}`
                    : 'ESP32 / Arduino Telemetry Endpoint'}
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                {targetMode === 'mission'
                  ? 'Opens this exact Martian territory straight into its 3D 360Â° surface panorama and NASA mission dossier.'
                  : targetMode === 'rover'
                    ? 'Launches directly into real-time WebSocket rover tracking and anomaly alerts for field testing.'
                    : 'Point your hardware configuration portal or companion device to this live JSON ingestion endpoint.'}
              </p>
              <div className="font-mono-tabular text-[11px] text-amber-300 break-all bg-[#090C12] border border-white/10 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded">
                {targetUrl}
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleCopy}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-medium border border-white/15 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-slate-100 transition-colors"
              >
                {copied ? (
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                ) : (
                  <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                )}
                <span>{copied ? 'Link Copied' : 'Copy Target URL'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Compass,
  Expand,
  Globe,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  Layers,
  QrCode,
  ShieldAlert,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { DataLayerId, MarsSite, SiteGalleryImage } from '../types/mars';
import { assessSiteSafety } from '../utils/marsLogic';
import { EvaRouteMap } from './EvaRouteMap';

interface SiteDossierPanelProps {
  site: MarsSite;
  allSites: MarsSite[];
  activeLayer: DataLayerId;
  onSelectSite: (id: string) => void;
  onOpenPanorama: () => void;
  onSwitchToRover: () => void;
  onOpenQr: () => void;
}

type DossierTab = 'telemetry' | 'route' | 'imagery';

function ThresholdBar({
  value,
  max,
  warnAt,
  dangerAt,
}: {
  value: number;
  max: number;
  warnAt: number;
  dangerAt: number;
}) {
  const pct = Math.max(4, Math.min(100, (value / max) * 100));
  const barColor =
    value >= dangerAt
      ? 'bg-rose-500'
      : value >= warnAt
        ? 'bg-amber-400'
        : 'bg-emerald-400';

  return (
    <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-slate-800/90 rounded-xs overflow-hidden mt-2">
      <div
        className={`h-full ${barColor} transition-all duration-300`}
        style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export function SiteDossierPanel({
  site,
  allSites,
  activeLayer,
  onSelectSite,
  onOpenPanorama,
  onSwitchToRover,
  onOpenQr,
}: SiteDossierPanelProps) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<DossierTab>('telemetry');
  const [selectedPhotoIdx, setSelectedPhotoIdx] = useState<number>(0);
  const [lightboxImg, setLightboxImg] = useState<SiteGalleryImage | null>(null);
  const [imgFailed, setImgFailed] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const safety = assessSiteSafety(site);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedPhotoIdx(0);
  }, [site.id]);

  const activePhoto = site.gallery[selectedPhotoIdx] ?? site.gallery[0];

  const verdictConfig =
    safety.verdict === 'SAFE'
      ? {
          icon: CheckCircle2,
          label: 'LOW REGIONAL SLOPE Â· LOCAL DEM CHECK',
          cls: 'border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-950/30 text-emerald-300',
        }
      : safety.verdict === 'CAUTION'
        ? {
            icon: AlertTriangle,
            label: 'TERRAIN CAUTION Â· SLOPE & MOBILITY FACTORS',
            cls: 'border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-950/30 text-amber-300',
          }
        : {
            icon: ShieldAlert,
            label: 'HIGH RELIEF Â· ROBOTIC SCOUT RECOMMENDED',
            cls: 'border-rose-500/40 bg-rose-950/30 text-rose-300',
          };

  const VerdictIcon = verdictConfig.icon;
  const o2Trace = Math.max(0.12, +(100 - site.atmosphereMix.co2 - site.atmosphereMix.n2 - site.atmosphereMix.ar).toFixed(2));

                    </div>

          </div>
        )}

      {/* Footer Action to Deploy Live Rover at This Site */}

      {/* Fullscreen NASA Imagery Lightbox */}
      {lightboxImg && (
        <div

        >
          <div className="w-full max-w-4xl border border-white/20 bg-[#090C12] rounded overflow-hidden shadow-2xl">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-white/10">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-slate-100">
                  {site.name} â€” {lightboxImg.title}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                  {lightboxImg.source} Â· {lightboxImg.instrument}
                </p>

              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />

}

/**
 * @license
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

class CinematicAudioEngine {
  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
  private masterGain: GainNode | null = null;
  private ambienceGain: GainNode | null = null;
  private sfxGain: GainNode | null = null;

  // Active sound sources
  private windNode: AudioBufferSourceNode | null = null;
  private lfoNode: OscillatorNode | null = null;
  private currentVoiceAudio: HTMLAudioElement | null = null;
  private isMuted = false;
  private userUnlocked = false;

  // Local audio overrides registry (drop-in custom voiceover/SFX mapping)
  private audioOverrides = new Map<string, string>();

  private initContext() {
    if (this.ctx && this.ctx.state !== 'closed') {
      if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {
        void this.ctx.resume();
      }
      return;
    }

    const AudioContextClass =
      window.AudioContext ||
      (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;

    if (!AudioContextClass) return;

    this.ctx = new AudioContextClass();

    this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
    this.masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(this.isMuted ? 0 : 0.85, this.ctx.currentTime);
    this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);

    this.ambienceGain = this.ctx.createGain();
    this.ambienceGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.7, this.ctx.currentTime);
    this.ambienceGain.connect(this.masterGain);

    this.sfxGain = this.ctx.createGain();
    this.sfxGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.9, this.ctx.currentTime);
    this.sfxGain.connect(this.masterGain);
  }

  public unlock() {
    this.userUnlocked = true;
    this.initContext();
    if (this.ctx && this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {
      void this.ctx.resume();
    }
  }

  public setMuted(muted: boolean) {
    this.isMuted = muted;
    if (this.masterGain && this.ctx) {
      this.masterGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(muted ? 0 : 0.85, this.ctx.currentTime, 0.05);
    }
    if (this.currentVoiceAudio) {
      this.currentVoiceAudio.muted = muted;
    }
  }

  // --- Local Audio Overrides Registry ---
  public registerAudioOverride(key: string, audioUrl: string): void {
    this.audioOverrides.set(key.toLowerCase(), audioUrl);
  }

  public registerAudioOverrides(overrides: Record<string, string>): void {
    for (const [key, url] of Object.entries(overrides)) {
      this.audioOverrides.set(key.toLowerCase(), url);
    }
  }

  public getAudioOverride(key: string): string | null {
    return this.audioOverrides.get(key.toLowerCase()) ?? null;
  }

  public hasAudioOverride(key: string): boolean {
    return this.audioOverrides.has(key.toLowerCase());
  }

  public resolveOverride(chapterId: string, beatId: string, lang: string): string | null {
    const keysToTry = [
      `${beatId}-${lang}`,
      `${beatId}_${lang}`,
      `${chapterId}-${beatId}-${lang}`,
      `${chapterId}_${beatId}_${lang}`,
      beatId,
    ];
    for (const k of keysToTry) {
      const match = this.getAudioOverride(k);
      if (match) return match;
    }
    return null;
  }

  // --- Procedural Martian Wind Ambience ---
  public startAmbience() {
    this.initContext();
    if (!this.ctx || !this.ambienceGain || this.windNode) return;

    try {
      // 5-second loop of filtered pink/brownian noise
      const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 5;
      const noiseBuffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
      const output = noiseBuffer.getChannelData(0);
      let b0 = 0,
        b1 = 0,
        b2 = 0;

      for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) {
        const white = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
        // Pink-like filter
        b0 = 0.99765 * b0 + white * 0.099046;
        b1 = 0.963 * b1 + white * 0.101;
        b2 = 0.57 * b2 + white * 0.55;
        output[i] = (b0 + b1 + b2) * 0.12;
      }

      const whiteNoise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
      whiteNoise.buffer = noiseBuffer;
      whiteNoise.loop = true;

      // Lowpass resonant wind filter
      const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
      filter.type = 'lowpass';
      filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(320, this.ctx.currentTime);
      filter.Q.setValueAtTime(4.2, this.ctx.currentTime);

      // Slow LFO for gusting wind dynamics
      const lfo = this.ctx.createOscillator();
      lfo.frequency.setValueAtTime(0.18, this.ctx.currentTime);
      const lfoGain = this.ctx.createGain();
      lfoGain.gain.setValueAtTime(140, this.ctx.currentTime);
      lfo.connect(lfoGain);
      lfoGain.connect(filter.frequency);

      whiteNoise.connect(filter);
      filter.connect(this.ambienceGain);

      whiteNoise.start();
      lfo.start();
      this.windNode = whiteNoise;
      this.lfoNode = lfo;
    } catch {
      // Audio autoplay limitation
    }
  }

  public stopAmbience() {
    if (this.windNode) {
      try {
        this.windNode.stop();
        this.windNode.disconnect();
      } catch {
        // Ignored
      }
      this.windNode = null;
    }
    if (this.lfoNode) {
      try {
        this.lfoNode.stop();
        this.lfoNode.disconnect();
      } catch {
        // Ignored
      }
      this.lfoNode = null;
    }
  }

  // --- Dynamic Audio Ducking ---
  public duckAmbience(targetGain = 0.18, rampSec = 0.25) {
    if (this.ambienceGain && this.ctx) {
      this.ambienceGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(targetGain, this.ctx.currentTime, rampSec);
    }
  }

  public restoreAmbience(targetGain = 0.7, rampSec = 0.35) {
    if (this.ambienceGain && this.ctx) {
      this.ambienceGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(targetGain, this.ctx.currentTime, rampSec);
    }
  }

  // --- Tension Riser for Atmospheric Descent and EVA Decisions ---
  public playRiser(durationSec = 3.2) {
    this.initContext();
    if (!this.ctx || !this.sfxGain || this.isMuted) return;

    try {
      const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
      const osc1 = this.ctx.createOscillator();
      const osc2 = this.ctx.createOscillator();
      const riserGain = this.ctx.createGain();
      const bpf = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();

      osc1.type = 'sawtooth';
      osc2.type = 'triangle';

      // Pitch sweep from sub-bass to tension treble
      osc1.frequency.setValueAtTime(68, now);
      osc1.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(520, now + durationSec);

      osc2.frequency.setValueAtTime(136, now);
      osc2.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1040, now + durationSec);

      bpf.type = 'bandpass';
      bpf.frequency.setValueAtTime(180, now);
      bpf.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1800, now + durationSec);
      bpf.Q.setValueAtTime(3.5, now);

      // Crescendo volume ramp
      riserGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.01, now);
      riserGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.48, now + durationSec * 0.88);
      riserGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + durationSec + 0.15);

      osc1.connect(bpf);
      osc2.connect(bpf);
      bpf.connect(riserGain);
      riserGain.connect(this.sfxGain);

      osc1.start(now);
      osc2.start(now);
      osc1.stop(now + durationSec + 0.2);
      osc2.stop(now + durationSec + 0.2);
    } catch {
      // Ignored
    }
  }

  // --- Discovery Swell for Scientific Breakthroughs & Orbital Reveals ---
  public playDiscoverySwell(durationSec = 3.8) {
    this.initContext();
    if (!this.ctx || !this.sfxGain || this.isMuted) return;

    try {
      const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
      const freqs = [146.83, 220.0, 293.66, 440.0]; // D minor / planetary resonance
      const swellGain = this.ctx.createGain();
      swellGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, now);
      swellGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.38, now + durationSec * 0.4);
      swellGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + durationSec);

      const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
      filter.type = 'lowpass';
      filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(350, now);
      filter.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(2600, now + durationSec * 0.5);
      filter.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(450, now + durationSec);
      filter.Q.setValueAtTime(2.5, now);

      for (const f of freqs) {
        const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
        osc.type = 'triangle';
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(f, now);
        osc.connect(filter);
        osc.start(now);
        osc.stop(now + durationSec + 0.1);
      }

      filter.connect(swellGain);
      swellGain.connect(this.sfxGain);
    } catch {
      // Ignored
    }
  }

  // --- Radio Comm Squelch Click ---
  public playRadioSquelch() {
    this.initContext();
    if (!this.ctx || !this.sfxGain || this.isMuted) return;

    try {
      const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
      const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = this.ctx.createGain();

      osc.type = 'square';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, now);
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(660, now + 0.04);

      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, now);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.09);

      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(this.sfxGain);

      osc.start(now);
      osc.stop(now + 0.1);
    } catch {
      // Ignored
    }
  }

  // --- Decision Confirmation Chime ---
  public playDecisionSting(isHazard = false) {
    this.initContext();
    if (!this.ctx || !this.sfxGain || this.isMuted) return;

    try {
      const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
      const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = this.ctx.createGain();

      osc.type = isHazard ? 'sawtooth' : 'sine';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(isHazard ? 240 : 540, now);
      osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(isHazard ? 180 : 880, now + 0.18);

      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.25, now);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.35);

      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(this.sfxGain);

      osc.start(now);
      osc.stop(now + 0.36);
    } catch {
      // Ignored
    }
  }

  // --- Atmospheric Entry & Rocket Thruster Rumble ---
  public playThrusterBurn(durationSec = 4.5) {
    this.initContext();
    if (!this.ctx || !this.sfxGain || this.isMuted) return;

    try {
      const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
      const subOsc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
      subOsc.type = 'sawtooth';
      subOsc.frequency.setValueAtTime(54, now);
      subOsc.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(38, now + durationSec);

      const lpf = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
      lpf.type = 'lowpass';
      lpf.frequency.setValueAtTime(160, now);
      lpf.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(110, now + durationSec);
      lpf.Q.setValueAtTime(3.8, now);

      const bufferSize = Math.floor(this.ctx.sampleRate * Math.min(durationSec, 6));
      const noiseBuffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
      const data = noiseBuffer.getChannelData(0);
      for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) {
        data[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
      }
      const noiseSource = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
      noiseSource.buffer = noiseBuffer;

      const noiseFilter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
      noiseFilter.type = 'bandpass';
      noiseFilter.frequency.setValueAtTime(240, now);
      noiseFilter.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(480, now + durationSec * 0.4);
      noiseFilter.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(180, now + durationSec);
      noiseFilter.Q.setValueAtTime(2.2, now);

      const thrusterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
      thrusterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.01, now);
      thrusterGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.55, now + 0.5);
      thrusterGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.58, now + durationSec * 0.7);
      thrusterGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + durationSec);

      subOsc.connect(lpf);
      lpf.connect(thrusterGain);
      noiseSource.connect(noiseFilter);
      noiseFilter.connect(thrusterGain);
      thrusterGain.connect(this.sfxGain);

      subOsc.start(now);
      noiseSource.start(now);
      subOsc.stop(now + durationSec + 0.1);
      noiseSource.stop(now + durationSec + 0.1);
    } catch {
      // Ignored
    }
  }

  // --- Supersonic Entry Shockwave (Sonic Boom) ---
  public playSonicBoom() {
    this.initContext();
    if (!this.ctx || !this.sfxGain || this.isMuted) return;

    try {
      const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
      for (const delay of [0, 0.08]) {
        const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        osc.type = 'sine';
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(110, now + delay);
        osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(24, now + delay + 0.32);

        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.42, now + delay);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + delay + 0.35);

        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.sfxGain);
        osc.start(now + delay);
        osc.stop(now + delay + 0.36);
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignored
    }
  }

  // --- Touchdown Hydraulic Shock & Magnetic Clamps ---
  public playTouchdown() {
    this.initContext();
    if (!this.ctx || !this.sfxGain || this.isMuted) return;

    try {
      const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
      const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = 'triangle';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(84, now);
      osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(32, now + 0.28);

      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.6, now);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.3);

      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(this.sfxGain);
      osc.start(now);
      osc.stop(now + 0.32);
    } catch {
      // Ignored
    }
  }

  // --- Cue Dispatcher ---
  public playCue(cue: string) {
    switch (cue) {
      case 'descent-riser':
        this.playRiser(3.2);
        break;
      case 'eva-tension':
        this.playRiser(2.6);
        break;
      case 'radio-comm':
        this.playRadioSquelch();
        break;
      case 'discovery-swell':
        this.playDiscoverySwell(3.8);
        break;
      case 'ambient-wind':
      default:
        this.startAmbience();
        break;
    }
  }

  // --- Voice-Over Narration Playback with Ducking ---
  public playVoiceover(audioSrc: string, onEnded?: () => void) {
    this.stopVoiceover();
    this.initContext();

    // Duck background ambience
    this.duckAmbience(0.18, 0.2);

    const audio = new Audio(audioSrc);
    audio.muted = this.isMuted;
    this.currentVoiceAudio = audio;

    const restore = () => {
      this.restoreAmbience(0.7, 0.4);
      onEnded?.();
    };

    audio.onended = restore;
    audio.onerror = restore;

    void audio.play().catch(() => {
      restore();
    });
  }

  public stopVoiceover() {
    if (this.currentVoiceAudio) {
      this.currentVoiceAudio.pause();
      this.currentVoiceAudio.currentTime = 0;
      this.currentVoiceAudio = null;
    }
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    }
    this.restoreAmbience(0.7, 0.2);
  }

  public pause() {
    if (this.currentVoiceAudio) {
      this.currentVoiceAudio.pause();
    }
  }

  public resume() {
    if (this.currentVoiceAudio && this.currentVoiceAudio.paused) {
      void this.currentVoiceAudio.play().catch(() => {});
    }
  }
}

export const cinematicAudio = new CinematicAudioEngine();

/**

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'motion/react';
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Compass,
  Globe2,
  Languages,
  Layers,
  Pause,
  Play,
  Volume2,
  VolumeX,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { MarsGlobe3D } from '../../components/MarsGlobe3D';
import { MarsPanorama360 } from '../../components/MarsPanorama360';
import { cinematicAudio } from '../audio/cinematicAudioEngine';
import { STORY_CHAPTERS } from '../data/storyChapters';
import { StoryChapterSelector } from './StoryChapterSelector';
import { StoryChoiceOverlay } from './StoryChoiceOverlay';
import type { MarsSite, RoverTelemetryState } from '../../types/mars';
import type {
  StoryChapter,
  StoryChoiceOption,
  StoryLanguage,
} from '../types';

interface MissionStoriesTheaterProps {
  allSites: MarsSite[];
  roverState: RoverTelemetryState;
  initialChapterId?: string;
  initialLanguage?: StoryLanguage;
  onExit: () => void;
  onSelectSiteInConsole?: (siteId: string) => void;
}

export function MissionStoriesTheater({
  allSites,
  roverState,
  initialChapterId = 'jezero',
  initialLanguage = 'en',
  onExit,
  onSelectSiteInConsole,
}: MissionStoriesTheaterProps) {
  const [activeChapterId, setActiveChapterId] = useState<string>(initialChapterId);
  const [currentBeatIndex, setCurrentBeatIndex] = useState<number>(0);
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState<StoryLanguage>(initialLanguage);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [isMuted, setIsMuted] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [selectorOpen, setSelectorOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Astronaut choices tracking
  const [selectedChoices, setSelectedChoices] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [completedChapters, setCompletedChapters] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [voiceLoading, setVoiceLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const activeChapter: StoryChapter = useMemo(
    () => STORY_CHAPTERS.find((c) => c.id === activeChapterId) ?? STORY_CHAPTERS[0],
    [activeChapterId]
  );

  const activeSite: MarsSite = useMemo(
    () => allSites.find((s) => s.id === activeChapter.siteId) ?? allSites[0],
    [allSites, activeChapter.siteId]
  );

  const currentBeat = activeChapter.beats[currentBeatIndex] ?? activeChapter.beats[0];
  const isArabic = language === 'ar';

  const beatTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const playTokenRef = useRef<number>(0);

  // Voiceover narration pipeline:
  // 1. Check local audio override drop-in
  // 2. Request Gemini server-side TTS endpoint (/api/stories/voiceover)
  // 3. Fallback seamlessly to Web Speech API (SpeechSynthesis)
  const speakBeatNarration = useCallback(
    async (narrationText: string, lang: StoryLanguage, onFinished?: () => void) => {
      const token = ++playTokenRef.current;

      // 1. Drop-in support for local audio overrides
      const localOverride = cinematicAudio.resolveOverride(
        activeChapter.id,
        currentBeat.id,
        lang
      );
      if (localOverride) {
        setVoiceLoading(false);
        cinematicAudio.playVoiceover(localOverride, onFinished);
        return;
      }

      setVoiceLoading(true);

      // 2. Gemini TTS server endpoint with in-memory caching
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/stories/voiceover', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ text: narrationText, language: lang }),
        });

        if (token !== playTokenRef.current) return;

        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data?.ok && typeof data.audio === 'string') {
            setVoiceLoading(false);
            cinematicAudio.playVoiceover(data.audio, onFinished);
            return;
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // Network or server error - continue seamlessly to client-side fallback
      }

      if (token !== playTokenRef.current) return;
      setVoiceLoading(false);

      // 3. Client-side SpeechSynthesis fallback with procedural audio ducking
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(narrationText);
        utterance.lang = lang === 'ar' ? 'ar-SA' : 'en-US';
        utterance.rate = 0.95;

        // Attempt matching localized voice if available
        const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
        const matchedVoice = voices.find((v) =>
          lang === 'ar' ? v.lang.startsWith('ar') : v.lang.startsWith('en')
        );
        if (matchedVoice) {
          utterance.voice = matchedVoice;
        }

        // Procedural audio ducking during speech synthesis
        cinematicAudio.duckAmbience(0.18, 0.25);

        const restoreAndFinish = () => {
          cinematicAudio.restoreAmbience(0.7, 0.35);
          onFinished?.();
        };

        utterance.onend = restoreAndFinish;
        utterance.onerror = restoreAndFinish;

        window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
      } else {
        onFinished?.();
      }
    },
    [activeChapter.id, currentBeat.id]
  );

  // Handle current beat audio, sound cues, and narration flow
  useEffect(() => {
    cinematicAudio.unlock();
    cinematicAudio.startAmbience();

    // Trigger procedural audio cues based on current beat
    cinematicAudio.playCue(currentBeat.audioCue);

    const narration = isArabic ? currentBeat.narrationAr : currentBeat.narrationEn;
    const hasUnansweredChoice =
      currentBeat.choicePrompt && !selectedChoices[currentBeat.id];

    if (isPlaying) {
      void speakBeatNarration(narration, language, () => {
        // When narration finishes and beat has no pending choice, advance to next beat
        if (!hasUnansweredChoice && currentBeatIndex < activeChapter.beats.length - 1) {
          beatTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
            setCurrentBeatIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
          }, 2400);
        } else if (currentBeatIndex === activeChapter.beats.length - 1) {
          // Chapter completed
          setCompletedChapters((prev) =>
            prev.includes(activeChapter.id) ? prev : [...prev, activeChapter.id]
          );
        }
      });
    } else {
      cinematicAudio.stopVoiceover();
    }

    return () => {
      if (beatTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(beatTimerRef.current);
        beatTimerRef.current = null;
      }
      cinematicAudio.stopVoiceover();
    };
  }, [
    currentBeat,
    language,
    isPlaying,
    activeChapter,
    currentBeatIndex,
    isArabic,
    selectedChoices,
    speakBeatNarration,
  ]);

  // Handle astronaut choice selection
  const handleSelectOption = useCallback(
    (option: StoryChoiceOption) => {
      cinematicAudio.playDecisionSting(option.riskLevel === 'HAZARD');
      setSelectedChoices((prev) => ({ ...prev, [currentBeat.id]: option.id }));

      const consequence = isArabic ? option.consequenceAr : option.consequenceEn;
      void speakBeatNarration(consequence, language);
    },
    [currentBeat.id, isArabic, language, speakBeatNarration]
  );

  const handleContinueAfterChoice = useCallback(() => {
    if (currentBeatIndex < activeChapter.beats.length - 1) {
      setCurrentBeatIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
    }
  }, [currentBeatIndex, activeChapter.beats.length]);

  const handleTogglePlay = useCallback(() => {
    setIsPlaying((prev) => !prev);
  }, []);

  const handleToggleLanguage = useCallback(() => {
    setLanguage((prev) => (prev === 'en' ? 'ar' : 'en'));
  }, []);

  const handleToggleMute = useCallback(() => {
    setIsMuted((prev) => {
      const next = !prev;
      cinematicAudio.setMuted(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const handleSelectChapter = useCallback(
    (chapterId: string, initialLang?: StoryLanguage) => {
      setActiveChapterId(chapterId);
      setCurrentBeatIndex(0);
      if (initialLang) setLanguage(initialLang);
      setSelectorOpen(false);
      setIsPlaying(true);
    },
    []
  );

  const handlePrevBeat = useCallback(() => {
    setCurrentBeatIndex((prev) => Math.max(0, prev - 1));
  }, []);

  const handleNextBeat = useCallback(() => {
    setCurrentBeatIndex((prev) => Math.min(activeChapter.beats.length - 1, prev + 1));
  }, [activeChapter.beats.length]);

  return (
    <div
      dir={isArabic ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}
      className="relative h-screen w-screen overflow-hidden bg-[#020306] text-slate-100 flex flex-col select-none"
    >
      {/* 3D Backdrop Viewport (Seamless Three.js Globe or 360Â° Panorama with Framer Motion Crossfade) */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0 overflow-hidden">
        <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
          {currentBeat.stage === 'orbit' ? (
            <motion.div
              key="stage-orbit"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 1.04 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.96 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.75, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}
              className="absolute inset-0"
            >
              <MarsGlobe3D
                sites={allSites}
                selectedSiteId={activeSite.id}
                activeLayer="optical"
                roverState={roverState}
                onSelectSite={(id) => onSelectSiteInConsole?.(id)}
                showTerritories={true}
                viewportShiftX={0}
              />
            </motion.div>
          ) : (
            <motion.div
              key="stage-panorama"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 1.04 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.96 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.75, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}
              className="absolute inset-0"
            >
              <MarsPanorama360
                site={activeSite}
                allSites={allSites}
                onSelectSite={(id) => onSelectSiteInConsole?.(id)}
                onBackToGlobe={() => {}}
                hideChrome={true}
                cameraCue={currentBeat.cameraCue}
              />
            </motion.div>
          )}
        </AnimatePresence>

        {/* Cinematic Vignette Scrim */}
        <div
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#020306]/95 via-transparent to-[#020306]/85"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Top Aerospace Command Bar (Zone 1: Brand/Exit, Zone 2: Chapter Title, Zone 3: Controls) */}

        >
          <motion.p
            key={`${currentBeat.id}-${language}`}
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 6 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.25 }}
            className="font-display text-sm sm:text-base font-medium text-slate-100 leading-relaxed drop-shadow"
          >
            {isArabic ? currentBeat.subtitleAr : currentBeat.subtitleEn}
          </motion.p>

              disabled={currentBeatIndex === 0}
              aria-label={isArabic ? 'Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø´Ù‡Ø¯ Ø§Ù„Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ù‚' : 'Previous beat'}
              className="p-1.5 rounded border border-white/10 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:pointer-events-none text-slate-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
            >

              className="p-1.5 rounded border border-white/10 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:pointer-events-none text-slate-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
            >

                {isArabic ? 'Ø¬Ø§Ø±ÙŠ Ø¨Ø« Ø§Ù„ØªØ¹Ù„ÙŠÙ‚ Ø§Ù„ØµÙˆØªÙŠ...' : 'Streaming voiceover...'}
              </span>
            )}

          <div className="text-[11px] font-mono-tabular text-slate-400 text-center sm:text-right truncate max-w-md">
            <span>NASA Data Source: </span>
            <span className="text-slate-300">{currentBeat.sourceCitation}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>

      {/* Chapter Selection Modal Drawer */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {selectorOpen && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto"
          >
            <div className="relative w-full max-w-5xl bg-[#06090F] border border-white/15 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-2xl">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectorOpen(false)}
                className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-xs font-mono-tabular px-3 py-1.5 rounded border border-white/15 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-slate-200 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
              >
                {isArabic ? 'Ø¥ØºÙ„Ø§Ù‚' : 'Close'}
              </button>
              <StoryChapterSelector
                chapters={STORY_CHAPTERS}
                completedChapterIds={completedChapters}
                activeChapterId={activeChapter.id}
                language={language}
                onSelectChapter={handleSelectChapter}
                onClose={() => setSelectorOpen(false)}
              />
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * @license
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

import { motion } from 'motion/react';
import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, ShieldAlert, ArrowRight, Compass } from 'lucide-react';
import type { StoryChoiceOption, StoryChoicePrompt, StoryLanguage } from '../types';

interface StoryChoiceOverlayProps {
  prompt: StoryChoicePrompt;
  language: StoryLanguage;
  selectedChoiceId: string | null;
  onSelectOption: (option: StoryChoiceOption) => void;
  onContinue: () => void;
}

export function StoryChoiceOverlay({
  prompt,
  language,
  selectedChoiceId,
  onSelectOption,
  onContinue,
}: StoryChoiceOverlayProps) {
  const isArabic = language === 'ar';
  const selectedOption = prompt.options.find((opt) => opt.id === selectedChoiceId);

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 24, scale: 0.98 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
      exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -20, scale: 0.98 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.35, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}
      dir={isArabic ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}
      className="w-full max-w-2xl mx-auto border border-amber-500/40 bg-[#070A10]/95 backdrop-blur-md rounded-lg p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-2xl text-slate-100 space-y-4"
    >
      {/* Header Badge & Dilemma Question */}
      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono-tabular text-amber-400">
          <span className="uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Compass className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            {isArabic ? 'Ù†Ù‚Ø·Ø© Ù‚Ø±Ø§Ø± Ø§Ù„Ø³ÙŠØ± Ø§Ù„ÙØ¶Ø§Ø¦ÙŠ' : 'Critical EVA Decision Point'}
          </span>
          <span className="text-slate-400">
            {isArabic ? 'Ù…Ø¨Ù†ÙŠ Ø¹Ù„Ù‰ Ø¨ÙŠØ§Ù†Ø§Øª Ù†Ø§Ø³Ø§ Ø§Ù„Ù…ÙŠØ¯Ø§Ù†ÙŠØ©' : 'Grounded in NASA Field Data'}
          </span>
        </div>
        <h3 className="font-display text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold text-slate-50 leading-snug">
          {isArabic ? prompt.questionAr : prompt.questionEn}
        </h3>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
          {isArabic ? prompt.contextAr : prompt.contextEn}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Option Buttons */}
      {!selectedOption ? (
        <div className="space-y-2.5 pt-1">
          {prompt.options.map((opt) => {
            const isHazard = opt.riskLevel === 'HAZARD';
            const isCaution = opt.riskLevel === 'CAUTION';
            const riskBorder = isHazard
              ? 'hover:border-rose-500 hover:bg-rose-950/30'
              : isCaution
                ? 'hover:border-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-950/30'
                : 'hover:border-emerald-500 hover:bg-emerald-950/30';

            const riskBadge = isHazard
              ? 'text-rose-400 bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-500/40'
              : isCaution
                ? 'text-amber-400 bg-amber-500/15 border-amber-500/40'
                : 'text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-500/40';

            const RiskIcon = isHazard ? ShieldAlert : isCaution ? AlertTriangle : CheckCircle2;

            return (
              <button
                key={opt.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectOption(opt)}
                className={`w-full text-start p-3.5 rounded border border-white/15 bg-white/[0.03] transition-all flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400 ${riskBorder}`}
              >
                <div
                  className={`mt-0.5 p-1 rounded border shrink-0 ${riskBadge}`}
                  aria-hidden="true"
                >
                  <RiskIcon className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <span className="font-display text-sm font-semibold text-slate-100">
                      {isArabic ? opt.labelAr : opt.labelEn}
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={`text-[10px] font-mono-tabular px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border shrink-0 ${riskBadge}`}
                    >
                      {opt.riskLevel}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[11px] font-mono-tabular text-slate-400">
                    {opt.nasaDataCitation}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* Selected Option Consequence & Continue */
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          className="space-y-4 pt-1"
        >
          <div
            className={`p-4 rounded border space-y-2 ${
              selectedOption.riskLevel === 'HAZARD'
                ? 'border-rose-500/60 bg-rose-950/40 text-rose-100'
                : selectedOption.riskLevel === 'CAUTION'
                  ? 'border-amber-500/60 bg-amber-950/40 text-amber-100'
                  : 'border-emerald-500/60 bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-100'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-mono-tabular uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold">
              <span>{isArabic ? 'Ø§Ù„Ù†ØªÙŠØ¬Ø© Ø§Ù„Ù…ÙŠØ¯Ø§Ù†ÙŠØ©' : 'Field Consequence'}</span>
              <span>Â·</span>
              <span>{selectedOption.riskLevel}</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed">
              {isArabic ? selectedOption.consequenceAr : selectedOption.consequenceEn}
            </p>
            <div className="text-xs font-mono-tabular text-slate-300/80 pt-1 border-t border-white/10">
              {isArabic ? 'Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø±Ø¬Ø¹ Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ù„Ù…ÙŠ: ' : 'Source Citation: '}
              {selectedOption.nasaDataCitation}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onContinue}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-slate-950 font-display text-xs font-bold tracking-wide transition-colors cursor-pointer focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-300"
            >
              <span>{isArabic ? 'Ù…ØªØ§Ø¨Ø¹Ø© Ø§Ù„Ø³ÙŠØ± Ø§Ù„ÙØ¶Ø§Ø¦ÙŠ' : 'Continue Mission EVA'}</span>

}

export function getChapterById(id: string): StoryChapter | undefined {
  return STORY_CHAPTERS.find((c) => c.id === id);
}

export function getAllChapterPreviews(): {
  id: string;
  chapterNumber: string;
  titleEn: string;
  titleAr: string;
  subtitleEn: string;
  subtitleAr: string;
  heroImage: string;
  beatsCount: number;
}[] {
  return STORY_CHAPTERS.map((c) => ({
    id: c.id,
    chapterNumber: c.chapterNumber,
    titleEn: c.titleEn,
    titleAr: c.titleAr,
    subtitleEn: c.subtitleEn,
    subtitleAr: c.subtitleAr,
    heroImage: c.heroImage,
    beatsCount: c.beats.length,
  }));
}

/**
 * @license
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { STORY_CHAPTERS, getChapterById, getAllChapterPreviews } from './data/storyChapters.ts';
import type { StoryAudioCue } from './types.ts';

const VALID_AUDIO_CUES: StoryAudioCue[] = [
  'ambient-wind',
  'descent-riser',
  'eva-tension',
  'discovery-swell',
  'radio-comm',
];

test('STORY_CHAPTERS integrity and NASA grounding', () => {
  assert.equal(STORY_CHAPTERS.length, 5, 'Should have 5 NASA site chapters');

  const expectedIds = ['jezero', 'gale', 'meridiani', 'utopia', 'gusev'];
  for (const id of expectedIds) {
    const chapter = getChapterById(id);
    assert.ok(chapter, `Chapter ${id} must exist`);
    assert.equal(chapter.id, id);
    assert.ok(chapter.titleEn && chapter.titleAr, `Chapter ${id} must have EN and AR titles`);
    assert.ok(chapter.subtitleEn && chapter.subtitleAr, `Chapter ${id} must have EN and AR subtitles`);
    assert.ok(chapter.sources.length >= 4, `Chapter ${id} must cite at least 4 NASA sources`);
    assert.ok(chapter.elevationDatum.includes('MOLA'), `Chapter ${id} must reference authentic MOLA elevation datum`);

    // Must have at least 4 narrative beats
    assert.ok(chapter.beats.length >= 4, `Chapter ${id} must have at least 4 beats`);

    let choiceFound = false;
    for (const beat of chapter.beats) {
      assert.ok(beat.id, 'Beat must have id');
      assert.ok(beat.titleEn && beat.titleAr, 'Beat must have EN/AR titles');
      assert.ok(beat.narrationEn && beat.narrationAr, 'Beat must have EN/AR narration');
      assert.ok(beat.subtitleEn && beat.subtitleAr, 'Beat must have EN/AR subtitles');
      assert.ok(beat.cameraCue, 'Beat must define camera cues');
      assert.ok(VALID_AUDIO_CUES.includes(beat.audioCue), `Beat must define valid audio cue: ${beat.audioCue}`);
      assert.ok(beat.sourceCitation, 'Beat must cite source');

      if (beat.stage === 'orbit') {
        assert.ok(typeof beat.cameraCue.lat === 'number', 'Orbit beat must specify latitude');
        assert.ok(typeof beat.cameraCue.lon === 'number', 'Orbit beat must specify longitude');
        assert.ok(typeof beat.cameraCue.distance === 'number', 'Orbit beat must specify distance');
      } else if (beat.stage === 'panorama') {
        assert.ok(typeof beat.cameraCue.azimuthDeg === 'number', 'Panorama beat must specify azimuthDeg');
        assert.ok(typeof beat.cameraCue.elevationDeg === 'number', 'Panorama beat must specify elevationDeg');
      }

      if (beat.choicePrompt) {
        choiceFound = true;
        assert.ok(beat.choicePrompt.questionEn && beat.choicePrompt.questionAr, 'Choice must have EN/AR questions');
        assert.ok(beat.choicePrompt.contextEn && beat.choicePrompt.contextAr, 'Choice must have EN/AR context');
        assert.ok(beat.choicePrompt.options.length >= 3, `Choice for ${id} must feature 3 risk options (SAFE, CAUTION, HAZARD)`);

        const riskLevels = beat.choicePrompt.options.map((o) => o.riskLevel);
        assert.ok(riskLevels.includes('SAFE'), `${id} dilemma must include SAFE option`);
        assert.ok(riskLevels.includes('CAUTION'), `${id} dilemma must include CAUTION option`);
        assert.ok(riskLevels.includes('HAZARD'), `${id} dilemma must include HAZARD option`);

        for (const opt of beat.choicePrompt.options) {
          assert.ok(opt.labelEn && opt.labelAr, 'Option must have EN/AR label');
          assert.ok(opt.consequenceEn && opt.consequenceAr, 'Option must have EN/AR consequence');
          assert.ok(opt.nasaDataCitation, 'Option must cite empirical NASA datum');
          assert.ok(['SAFE', 'CAUTION', 'HAZARD'].includes(opt.riskLevel), 'Risk level must be valid');
          assert.ok(typeof opt.targetHeadingDeg === 'number', 'Option must define targetHeadingDeg');
          assert.ok(typeof opt.targetPitchDeg === 'number', 'Option must define targetPitchDeg');
        }
      }
    }
    assert.ok(choiceFound, `Chapter ${id} must feature an interactive astronaut decision`);
  }
});

    site.safetyVerdict === 'SAFE'

      : site.safetyVerdict === 'CAUTION'
        ? 'FIELD CAUTION Â· SLOPE & MOBILITY FACTORS'

const audioCache = new Map<string, string>();

export async function fetchDescentAudio(
  site: MarsSite,
  lang: 'ar' | 'en'
): Promise<string | null> {
  const { spokenText } = getDescentNarration(site, lang);
  const cacheKey = `${lang}:${site.id}`;
  if (audioCache.has(cacheKey)) {
    return audioCache.get(cacheKey)!;
  }

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/stories/voiceover', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ text: spokenText, language: lang }),
    });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok && typeof data.audio === 'string') {
      audioCache.set(cacheKey, data.audio);
      return data.audio;
    }
    return null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { MARS_SITES } from '../data/marsSites';
import {
  assessSiteSafety,
  computeRouteMetrics,
  evaluateRoverAnomalies,
  findSiteAtLatLon,
  isPointInPolygon,
  latLonToVector3,
  vector3ToLatLon,
} from './marsLogic';

test('latLonToVector3 and vector3ToLatLon round-trip accurately', () => {
  const radius = 5;
  for (const site of MARS_SITES) {
    const pt = latLonToVector3(site.lat, site.lon, radius);
    const magnitude = Math.hypot(pt.x, pt.y, pt.z);
    assert.ok(Math.abs(magnitude - radius) < 1e-6);

    const back = vector3ToLatLon(pt.x, pt.y, pt.z);
    assert.ok(Math.abs(back.lat - site.lat) < 1e-4);
    assert.ok(Math.abs(back.lon - site.lon) < 1e-4);
  }
});

test('territory polygons contain their center site coordinates and match via findSiteAtLatLon', () => {
  for (const site of MARS_SITES) {
    assert.ok(
      site.boundaryPolygon.length >= 6,
      `${site.id} should define a rich territory polygon`
    );
    assert.equal(
      isPointInPolygon(site.lat, site.lon, site.boundaryPolygon),
      true,
      `${site.id} polygon must contain its center`
    );
    const hit = findSiteAtLatLon(site.lat, site.lon, MARS_SITES);
    assert.equal(hit?.id, site.id);
  }

  // Open Mars region outside all 5 territories
  assert.equal(findSiteAtLatLon(-65, 20, MARS_SITES), null);
});

test('assessSiteSafety distinguishes SAFE and CAUTION terrain indices across the 5 NASA PDF sites', () => {
  const utopia = MARS_SITES.find((s) => s.id === 'utopia')!;
  const jezero = MARS_SITES.find((s) => s.id === 'jezero')!;
  const gale = MARS_SITES.find((s) => s.id === 'gale')!;
  const meridiani = MARS_SITES.find((s) => s.id === 'meridiani')!;
  const gusev = MARS_SITES.find((s) => s.id === 'gusev')!;

  const utopiaResult = assessSiteSafety(utopia);
  assert.equal(utopiaResult.verdict, 'SAFE');
  assert.ok(utopiaResult.score >= 80);

  for (const s of [jezero, gale, meridiani, gusev]) {
    const res = assessSiteSafety(s);
    assert.ok(res.verdict === 'CAUTION' || res.verdict === 'HAZARDOUS');
  }
});

test('evaluateRoverAnomalies returns empty when nominal and triggers all 3 anomaly types on spikes', () => {
  const gale = MARS_SITES.find((s) => s.id === 'gale')!;

  const nominal = evaluateRoverAnomalies(

    1000

  assert.equal(rockAnomaly[0].type, 'OBSTACLE_DETECTED');

    gale,
    1000
  );
  assert.equal(multiAnomaly.length, 3);

  assert.ok(types.includes('OBSTACLE_DETECTED'));
  assert.ok(types.includes('SLOPE_ANOMALY'));
  assert.ok(types.includes('CO2_ANOMALY'));
});

--- FILE: C:\Users\EL Hramain\Downloads\areswalk-mars-eva-rover-telemetry-nov-14-2026-5-48-44-pm\src\utils\marsLogic.ts ---

import type {
  AnomalyAlert,
  EvaWaypoint,
  MarsSite,
  SafetyVerdict,
  TerrainHazard,
} from '../types/mars';

export interface CartesianPoint {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  z: number;
}

/**
 * Converts Martian latitude (-90..90) and East longitude (0..360)
 * onto a 3D sphere matching Three.js equirectangular UV mapping.
 */
export function latLonToVector3(
  latDeg: number,
  lonDeg: number,
  radius: number
): CartesianPoint {
  const phi = ((90 - latDeg) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const theta = ((lonDeg + 180) * Math.PI) / 180;

  const x = -radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);
  const y = radius * Math.cos(phi);
  const z = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);

  return { x, y, z };
}

/**
 * Inverse projection from a 3D point on the Mars sphere back to Martian
 * latitude (-90..90) and East longitude (0..360).
 */
export function vector3ToLatLon(
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number
): { lat: number; lon: number } {
  const r = Math.hypot(x, y, z) || 1;
  const lat = 90 - (Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, y / r))) * 180) / Math.PI;
  let theta = (Math.atan2(z, -x) * 180) / Math.PI;
  if (theta < 0) theta += 360;
  let lon = theta - 180;
  lon = ((lon % 360) + 360) % 360;
  return { lat, lon };
}

/**
 * Ray-casting point-in-polygon check on geographic [lat, lon] coordinates.
 */
export function isPointInPolygon(
  lat: number,
  lon: number,
  polygon: [number, number][]
): boolean {
  if (polygon.length < 3) return false;
  const normLon = ((lon % 360) + 360) % 360;

  // Unwrap longitudes relative to first vertex to prevent 0/360 seam splits
  const baseLon = polygon[0][1];
  const unwrap = (l: number) => {
    let d = ((l - baseLon + 540) % 360) - 180;
    return baseLon + d;
  };
  const testLon = unwrap(normLon);

  let inside = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = polygon.length - 1; i < polygon.length; j = i++) {
    const xi = polygon[i][0];
    const yi = unwrap(polygon[i][1]);
    const xj = polygon[j][0];
    const yj = unwrap(polygon[j][1]);

    const intersect =
      yi > testLon !== yj > testLon &&
      lat < ((xj - xi) * (testLon - yi)) / (yj - yi || 1e-9) + xi;
    if (intersect) inside = !inside;
  }
  return inside;
}

/**
 * Finds the Martian region territory containing the clicked lat/lon coordinate.
 */
export function findSiteAtLatLon(
  lat: number,
  lon: number,
  sites: MarsSite[]
): MarsSite | null {
  for (const site of sites) {
    if (isPointInPolygon(lat, lon, site.boundaryPolygon)) {
      return site;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export interface SiteSafetyAssessment {
  verdict: SafetyVerdict;
  score: number; // 0..100
  factors: string[];
}

export function assessSiteSafety(site: MarsSite): SiteSafetyAssessment {
  let score = 100;
  const factors: string[] = [];

  if (site.meanSlopeDeg > 20) {
    score -= 42;
    factors.push(`Extreme slope (${site.meanSlopeDeg.toFixed(1)}Â° mean)`);
  } else if (site.meanSlopeDeg > 10) {
    score -= 22;
    factors.push(`Steep ascent terrain (${site.meanSlopeDeg.toFixed(1)}Â° mean)`);
  } else if (site.meanSlopeDeg > 5) {
    score -= 10;
    factors.push(`Moderate relief (${site.meanSlopeDeg.toFixed(1)}Â° mean)`);
  }

  if (site.radiationMsvDay >= 1.0) {
    score -= 28;
    factors.push(`High radiation dose (${site.radiationMsvDay.toFixed(2)} mSv/day)`);
  } else if (site.radiationMsvDay >= 0.65) {
    score -= 8;
  }

  if (site.pressureKpa < 0.2) {
    score -= 22;
    factors.push(`Ultra-thin atmosphere (${site.pressureKpa.toFixed(2)} kPa)`);
  }

  if (site.dustOpacityTau >= 0.9) {
    score -= 18;
    factors.push(`High dust opacity (Tau ${site.dustOpacityTau.toFixed(2)})`);
  } else if (site.dustOpacityTau >= 0.7) {
    score -= 8;
  }

  const clampedScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(score)));
  let verdict: SafetyVerdict = 'SAFE';
  if (clampedScore < 50 || site.meanSlopeDeg > 18 || site.pressureKpa < 0.2) {
    verdict = 'HAZARDOUS';
  } else if (clampedScore < 80 || site.meanSlopeDeg > 5) {
    verdict = 'CAUTION';
  }

  return { verdict, score: clampedScore, factors };
}

};

      detailAr: `Ø§Ù†Ø­Ø¯Ø§Ø± Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ø±Ø¨Ø© Ø§Ù„Ø­Ø§Ù„ÙŠ ${maxTilt.toFixed(1)}Â° ÙŠØªØ¬Ø§ÙˆØ² Ø®Ø· Ø£Ø³Ø§Ø³ Ù†Ø§Ø³Ø§ (${site.meanSlopeDeg.toFixed(1)}Â°).`,
      measuredValue: `${maxTilt.toFixed(1)}Â° tilt`,
      nasaBaseline: `${site.meanSlopeDeg.toFixed(1)}Â° MOLA`,
      timestamp: now,
    });
  }

  const co2Ratio = sensor.co2Ppm / Math.max(1, site.co2BaselinePpm);
  if (
    sensor.co2Ppm >= ROVER_THRESHOLDS.co2AbsoluteWarningPpm ||
    co2Ratio >= ROVER_THRESHOLDS.co2SpikeMultiplier
  ) {
    const critical = sensor.co2Ppm >= ROVER_THRESHOLDS.co2AbsoluteCriticalPpm;
    alerts.push({
      id: 'anomaly-co2',
      type: 'CO2_ANOMALY',
      severity: critical ? 'CRITICAL' : 'WARNING',
      title: critical
        ? 'CRITICAL CO2 SPIKE: Atmospheric Plume Detected'

      titleAr: critical
