        : 'ØªÙ†Ø¨ÙŠÙ‡ ØºØ§Ø²: ØªØºÙŠØ± Ù…ÙØ§Ø¬Ø¦ ÙÙŠ Ù†Ø³Ø¨Ø© Ø«Ø§Ù†ÙŠ Ø£ÙƒØ³ÙŠØ¯ Ø§Ù„ÙƒØ±Ø¨ÙˆÙ†',
      detail: `Live CO2 sensor spiked to ${Math.round(sensor.co2Ppm)} ppm (${co2Ratio.toFixed(1)}Ã— local reference ${site.co2BaselinePpm} ppm / ${site.co2Percent}% Mars atm).`,
      detailAr: `Ù‚Ø±Ø§Ø¡Ø© Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø³ Ø«Ø§Ù†ÙŠ Ø£ÙƒØ³ÙŠØ¯ Ø§Ù„ÙƒØ±Ø¨ÙˆÙ† Ù‚ÙØ²Øª Ø¥Ù„Ù‰ ${Math.round(sensor.co2Ppm)} ppm Ù…Ù‚Ø§Ø±Ù†Ø© Ø¨Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø±Ø¬Ø¹ (${site.co2BaselinePpm} ppm).`,
      measuredValue: `${Math.round(sensor.co2Ppm)} ppm`,
      nasaBaseline: `${site.co2BaselinePpm} ppm ref (${site.co2Percent}% atm)`,
      timestamp: now,

  }

  return alerts;
}

export interface RouteMetrics {
  totalDistanceM: number;
  estimatedEvaMinutes: number;
  minHazardClearanceM: number;
  elevationGainM: number;
}

export function computeRouteMetrics(
  waypoints: EvaWaypoint[],
  hazards: TerrainHazard[]
): RouteMetrics {
  if (waypoints.length === 0) {
    return {
      totalDistanceM: 0,
      estimatedEvaMinutes: 0,
      minHazardClearanceM: 0,
      elevationGainM: 0,
    };
  }

  let totalDistanceM = 0;
  let elevationGainM = 0;

  for (let i = 1; i < waypoints.length; i++) {
    const prev = waypoints[i - 1];
    const curr = waypoints[i];
    totalDistanceM += Math.hypot(curr.x - prev.x, curr.y - prev.y);
    const dElev = curr.elevationOffsetM - prev.elevationOffsetM;
    if (dElev > 0) elevationGainM += dElev;
  }

  let minHazardClearanceM = Infinity;
  if (hazards.length > 0 && waypoints.length > 1) {
    for (let i = 1; i < waypoints.length; i++) {
      const a = waypoints[i - 1];
      const b = waypoints[i];
      for (const h of hazards) {
        const distToSeg = pointToSegmentDistance(h.x, h.y, a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y);
        const clearance = distToSeg - h.radiusM;
        if (clearance < minHazardClearanceM) {
          minHazardClearanceM = clearance;
        }
      }
    }
  } else {
    minHazardClearanceM = 50;
  }

  const suitSpeedMs = 0.75;
  const estimatedEvaMinutes = Math.max(1, Math.round(totalDistanceM / suitSpeedMs / 60));

  return {
    totalDistanceM: Math.round(totalDistanceM * 10) / 10,
    estimatedEvaMinutes,
    minHazardClearanceM: Math.round(Math.max(0, minHazardClearanceM) * 10) / 10,
    elevationGainM: Math.round(elevationGainM),
  };
}

function pointToSegmentDistance(
  px: number,
  py: number,
  ax: number,
  ay: number,
  bx: number,
  by: number
): number {
  const vx = bx - ax;
  const vy = by - ay;
  const lenSq = vx * vx + vy * vy;
  if (lenSq === 0) return Math.hypot(px - ax, py - ay);
  const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((px - ax) * vx + (py - ay) * vy) / lenSq));
  const projX = ax + t * vx;
  const projY = ay + t * vy;
  return Math.hypot(px - projX, py - projY);
}

import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { DataLayerId, MarsSite } from '../types/mars';

function hash2(x: number, y: number): number {
  const n = Math.sin(x * 127.1 + y * 311.7) * 43758.5453123;
  return n - Math.floor(n);
}

function valueNoise(x: number, y: number): number {
  const ix = Math.floor(x);
  const iy = Math.floor(y);
  const fx = x - ix;
  const fy = y - iy;
  const ux = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx);
  const uy = fy * fy * (3 - 2 * fy);
  const a = hash2(ix, iy);
  const b = hash2(ix + 1, iy);
  const c = hash2(ix, iy + 1);
  const d = hash2(ix + 1, iy + 1);
  return a + (b - a) * ux + (c - a) * uy + (a - b - c + d) * ux * uy;
}

function fbm(x: number, y: number, octaves = 4): number {
  let sum = 0;
  let amp = 0.5;
  let freq = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
    sum += amp * valueNoise(x * freq, y * freq);
    freq *= 2.12;
    amp *= 0.5;
  }
  return sum;
}

function angularDist(lat1: number, lon1: number, lat2: number, lon2: number): number {
  const dLat = lat1 - lat2;
  let dLon = Math.abs(lon1 - lon2);
  if (dLon > 180) dLon = 360 - dLon;
  return Math.hypot(dLat, dLon * Math.cos((lat1 * Math.PI) / 180));
}

interface CraterDef {
  lat: number;
  lon: number;
  rad: number;
  depth: number;
  centralPeak: boolean;
}

// 340 multi-scale Martian impact craters, biased toward rugged Southern Highlands
const CRATERS: CraterDef[] = Array.from({ length: 340 }, (_, i) => {
  const isLarge = i < 26;
  const isMedium = i >= 26 && i < 115;
  const southBias = hash2(i * 4.3, 8.1) < 0.68 ? -1 : 1;
  const rawLat = southBias * Math.pow(hash2(i * 3.1, 1.7), 0.78) * 74;
  const rad = isLarge
    ? 3.8 + hash2(i * 2.3, 9.1) * 5.2
    : isMedium
      ? 1.5 + hash2(i * 2.3, 9.1) * 2.3
      : 0.45 + hash2(i * 2.3, 9.1) * 1.1;

  return {
    lat: rawLat,
    lon: hash2(i * 7.3, 4.9) * 360,
    rad,
    depth: isLarge
      ? 0.25 + hash2(i * 5.7, 2.2) * 0.26
      : isMedium
        ? 0.16 + hash2(i * 5.7, 2.2) * 0.18
        : 0.1 + hash2(i * 5.7, 2.2) * 0.14,
    centralPeak: isLarge && hash2(i * 9.7, 3.3) > 0.35,
  };
});

const TEX_W = 2048;
const TEX_H = 1024;

interface CachedBaseField {
  heights: Float32Array;
  slopes: Float32Array;
  bumpMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  normalMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  roughnessMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
}

let cachedBaseField: CachedBaseField | null = null;
let cachedSkyTex: THREE.CanvasTexture | null = null;
const cachedLayerColorMaps = new Map<DataLayerId, THREE.CanvasTexture>();

function buildMacroHeight(latDeg: number, lonNorm: number): number {
  let h = Math.tanh((14 - latDeg) / 9.5) * 0.26 - 0.08;

  const nx = (lonNorm / 360) * 12;
  const ny = ((latDeg + 90) / 180) * 6;
  h += (fbm(nx, ny, 4) - 0.5) * 0.56;
  h += (fbm(nx * 4.2 + 13, ny * 4.2 + 7, 3) - 0.5) * 0.24;

  // Olympus Mons (18.65N, 226.2E) with nested caldera
  const dOlympus = angularDist(latDeg, lonNorm, 18.65, 226.2);
  if (dOlympus < 16) {
    h += Math.exp(-(dOlympus * dOlympus) / 36) * 1.28;
    if (dOlympus < 2.1) h -= 0.28;
    if (dOlympus > 7.5 && dOlympus < 9.5) h -= 0.06;
  }

  // Tharsis Montes trio
  const tharsisPeaks: [number, number][] = [
    [11.9, 255.9],
    [1.5, 247.0],
    [-8.3, 239.9],
  ];
  for (const [pLat, pLon] of tharsisPeaks) {
    const d = angularDist(latDeg, lonNorm, pLat, pLon);
    if (d < 9) {
      h += Math.exp(-(d * d) / 15) * 0.78;
      if (d < 1.2) h -= 0.18;
    }
  }

  // Tharsis Bulge
  const dTharsis = Math.hypot((latDeg - 1) * 1.1, (lonNorm - 250) * 0.65);
  if (dTharsis < 36) {
    h += Math.exp(-(dTharsis * dTharsis) / 420) * 0.56;
  }

  // Elysium Mons
  const dElysium = angularDist(latDeg, lonNorm, 25.0, 147.2);
  if (dElysium < 11) {
    h += Math.exp(-(dElysium * dElysium) / 24) * 0.68;
    if (dElysium < 1.1) h -= 0.14;
  }

  // Valles Marineris rift canyon + Candor/Melas spur + Noctis Labyrinthus
  if (lonNorm > 266 && lonNorm < 328 && Math.abs(latDeg + 13.5) < 12) {
    const wiggle = Math.sin((lonNorm - 270) * 0.24) * 1.15;
    const centerLat = -13.5 + wiggle;
    const mainMask =
      Math.exp(-Math.pow((latDeg - centerLat) / 1.9, 2)) *
      Math.sin(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (lonNorm - 272) / 52)) * Math.PI);
    const spurMask =
      lonNorm > 282 && lonNorm < 306
        ? Math.exp(-Math.pow((latDeg - (centerLat + 2.8)) / 1.1, 2)) *
          Math.sin(((lonNorm - 282) / 24) * Math.PI) *
          0.62
        : 0;
    const noctisMask =
      lonNorm > 266 && lonNorm < 280
        ? Math.exp(-Math.pow((latDeg + 8.5) / 3.2, 2)) *
          Math.abs(Math.sin(latDeg * 2.2 + lonNorm * 1.7)) *
          0.35
        : 0;
    h -= Math.max(mainMask, spurMask) * 0.98 + noctisMask;
  }

  // Kasei Valles outflow channel
  if (lonNorm > 285 && lonNorm < 306 && latDeg > 8 && latDeg < 28) {
    const channelCenter = 294 + (latDeg - 10) * 0.45;
    const dChannel = Math.abs(lonNorm - channelCenter);
    if (dChannel < 3.5) {
      h -= Math.exp(-(dChannel * dChannel) / 2.6) * 0.34;
    }
  }

  // Major basins (Hellas, Argyre, Isidis, Utopia)
  const dHellas = angularDist(latDeg, lonNorm, -42, 70);
  if (dHellas < 24) h -= Math.exp(-(dHellas * dHellas) / 160) * 0.84;

  const dArgyre = angularDist(latDeg, lonNorm, -50, 317);
  if (dArgyre < 14) h -= Math.exp(-(dArgyre * dArgyre) / 65) * 0.62;

  const dIsidis = angularDist(latDeg, lonNorm, 12.9, 87);
  if (dIsidis < 13) h -= Math.exp(-(dIsidis * dIsidis) / 55) * 0.42;

  const dUtopia = angularDist(latDeg, lonNorm, 47, 122);
  if (dUtopia < 24) h -= Math.exp(-(dUtopia * dUtopia) / 210) * 0.3;

  // 5 Landing site craters
  const dJezero = angularDist(latDeg, lonNorm, 18.44, 77.45);
  if (dJezero < 4.8) {
    h -= Math.exp(-(dJezero * dJezero) / 5.5) * 0.34;
    if (dJezero > 2.2 && dJezero < 3.6) h += 0.12;
  }

  const dGale = angularDist(latDeg, lonNorm, -5.4, 137.8);
  if (dGale < 5.2) {
    h -= Math.exp(-(dGale * dGale) / 6.5) * 0.42;
    if (dGale < 1.8) h += Math.exp(-(dGale * dGale) / 1.1) * 0.34;
  }

  const dEndeavour = angularDist(latDeg, lonNorm, -2.28, 354.78);
  if (dEndeavour < 3.5) {
    h -= Math.exp(-(dEndeavour * dEndeavour) / 3.4) * 0.22;
  }

  const dGusev = angularDist(latDeg, lonNorm, -14.59, 175.5);
  if (dGusev < 5.0) {
    h -= Math.exp(-(dGusev * dGusev) / 6.2) * 0.34;
    if (dGusev < 1.3) h += 0.14;
  }

  return h;
}

/**
 * Spatial-splatted heightfield generation: computes base fBm once per pixel and splats
 * 340 craters ONLY inside their local bounding rectangles (~5,000x faster than O(pixels * craters)).
 */
function getOrBuildBaseField(maxAnisotropy: number): CachedBaseField {
  if (cachedBaseField) return cachedBaseField;

  const heights = new Float32Array(TEX_W * TEX_H);
  for (let py = 0; py < TEX_H; py++) {
    const lat = 90 - (py / (TEX_H - 1)) * 180;
    for (let px = 0; px < TEX_W; px++) {
      const lonRaw = (px / (TEX_W - 1)) * 360 - 180;
      const lonNorm = lonRaw < 0 ? lonRaw + 360 : lonRaw;
      heights[py * TEX_W + px] = buildMacroHeight(lat, lonNorm);
    }
  }

  // Splat 340 multi-scale impact craters solely over their local bounding boxes
  for (let i = 0; i < CRATERS.length; i++) {
    const c = CRATERS[i];
    const cosLat = Math.max(0.22, Math.cos((c.lat * Math.PI) / 180));
    const latSpan = c.rad * 1.32;
    const lonSpan = (c.rad * 1.32) / cosLat;

    const pyMin = Math.max(0, Math.floor(((90 - (c.lat + latSpan)) / 180) * (TEX_H - 1)));
    const pyMax = Math.min(TEX_H - 1, Math.ceil(((90 - (c.lat - latSpan)) / 180) * (TEX_H - 1)));
    const pxSpan = Math.ceil((lonSpan / 360) * TEX_W);
    const centerLonWrapped = c.lon > 180 ? c.lon - 360 : c.lon;
    const pxCenter = Math.round(((centerLonWrapped + 180) / 360) * (TEX_W - 1));

    for (let py = pyMin; py <= pyMax; py++) {
      const lat = 90 - (py / (TEX_H - 1)) * 180;
      const dLat = lat - c.lat;
      for (let dx = -pxSpan; dx <= pxSpan; dx++) {
        const px = (pxCenter + dx + TEX_W) % TEX_W;
        const lonRaw = (px / (TEX_W - 1)) * 360 - 180;
        const lonNorm = lonRaw < 0 ? lonRaw + 360 : lonRaw;
        let dLon = Math.abs(lonNorm - c.lon);
        if (dLon > 180) dLon = 360 - dLon;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dLat, dLon * cosLat);
        if (dist < c.rad * 1.28) {
          const rNorm = dist / c.rad;
          let delta = 0;
          if (rNorm < 0.78) {
            const bowl = rNorm / 0.78;
            delta = -(1 - bowl * bowl * bowl) * c.depth * 0.72;
            if (c.centralPeak && rNorm < 0.22) {
              delta += Math.cos((rNorm / 0.22) * (Math.PI / 2)) * c.depth * 0.42;
            }
          } else {
            // Smooth weathered crater rim + gradual outer ejecta taper
            const ejectaT = (rNorm - 0.78) / 0.5;
            const rimBell = Math.sin(Math.min(1, ejectaT * 1.5) * Math.PI) * Math.pow(1 - ejectaT, 1.4);
            delta = rimBell * c.depth * 0.36;
          }
          heights[py * TEX_W + px] += delta;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < heights.length; i++) {
    if (heights[i] < -1.05) heights[i] = -1.05;
    else if (heights[i] > 1.28) heights[i] = 1.28;
  }

  const bumpCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  bumpCanvas.width = TEX_W;
  bumpCanvas.height = TEX_H;
  const bCtx = bumpCanvas.getContext('2d')!;
  const bImg = bCtx.createImageData(TEX_W, TEX_H);

  const normalCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  normalCanvas.width = TEX_W;
  normalCanvas.height = TEX_H;
  const nCtx = normalCanvas.getContext('2d')!;
  const nImg = nCtx.createImageData(TEX_W, TEX_H);

  const roughCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  roughCanvas.width = TEX_W;
  roughCanvas.height = TEX_H;
  const rCtx = roughCanvas.getContext('2d')!;
  const rImg = rCtx.createImageData(TEX_W, TEX_H);

  const slopes = new Float32Array(TEX_W * TEX_H);
  const normalStrength = 4.6;

  for (let py = 0; py < TEX_H; py++) {
    const lat = 90 - (py / (TEX_H - 1)) * 180;
    const cosLat = Math.max(0.25, Math.cos((lat * Math.PI) / 180));
    const pyUp = Math.max(0, py - 1);
    const pyDown = Math.min(TEX_H - 1, py + 1);

    for (let px = 0; px < TEX_W; px++) {
      const idx = py * TEX_W + px;
      const pxLeft = (px - 1 + TEX_W) % TEX_W;
      const pxRight = (px + 1) % TEX_W;

      const h = heights[idx];
      const hLeft = heights[py * TEX_W + pxLeft];
      const hRight = heights[py * TEX_W + pxRight];
      const hUp = heights[pyUp * TEX_W + px];
      const hDown = heights[pyDown * TEX_W + px];

      const dhDx = ((hRight - hLeft) * 0.5) / cosLat;
      const dhDy = (hDown - hUp) * 0.5;
      const slope = Math.min(1, Math.hypot(dhDx, dhDy) * 18);
      slopes[idx] = slope;

      let nx = -dhDx * normalStrength;
      let ny = dhDy * normalStrength;
      let nz = 1.0;
      const invLen = 1 / Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz);
      nx *= invLen;
      ny *= invLen;
      nz *= invLen;

      const micro = valueNoise(px * 0.42 + 20, py * 0.42 + 5);
      const o = idx * 4;

      const bumpVal = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round((h + 1) * 112 + micro * 30)));
      bImg.data[o] = bumpVal;
      bImg.data[o + 1] = bumpVal;
      bImg.data[o + 2] = bumpVal;
      bImg.data[o + 3] = 255;

      nImg.data[o] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round((nx * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255)));
      nImg.data[o + 1] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round((ny * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255)));
      nImg.data[o + 2] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round((nz * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255)));
      nImg.data[o + 3] = 255;

      const roughVal = Math.abs(lat) > 78 ? 170 : Math.max(115, Math.min(248, Math.round(196 + slope * 48)));
      rImg.data[o] = roughVal;
      rImg.data[o + 1] = roughVal;
      rImg.data[o + 2] = roughVal;
      rImg.data[o + 3] = 255;
    }
  }

  bCtx.putImageData(bImg, 0, 0);
  nCtx.putImageData(nImg, 0, 0);
  rCtx.putImageData(rImg, 0, 0);

  const bumpMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(bumpCanvas);
  bumpMap.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  bumpMap.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
  bumpMap.anisotropy = maxAnisotropy;

  const normalMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(normalCanvas);
  normalMap.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  normalMap.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
  normalMap.anisotropy = maxAnisotropy;

  const roughnessMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(roughCanvas);
  roughnessMap.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  roughnessMap.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
  roughnessMap.anisotropy = maxAnisotropy;

  cachedBaseField = { heights, slopes, bumpMap, normalMap, roughnessMap };
  return cachedBaseField;
}

export interface MarsSurfaceTextures {
  colorMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  bumpMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  normalMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  roughnessMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
}

export function createMarsSurfaceTextures(
  layer: DataLayerId,
  maxAnisotropy: number
): MarsSurfaceTextures {
  const base = getOrBuildBaseField(maxAnisotropy);
  const existingColor = cachedLayerColorMaps.get(layer);
  if (existingColor) {
    return {
      colorMap: existingColor,
      bumpMap: base.bumpMap,
      normalMap: base.normalMap,
      roughnessMap: base.roughnessMap,
    };
  }

  const colorCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  colorCanvas.width = TEX_W;
  colorCanvas.height = TEX_H;
  const cCtx = colorCanvas.getContext('2d')!;
  const cImg = cCtx.createImageData(TEX_W, TEX_H);

  for (let py = 0; py < TEX_H; py++) {
    const lat = 90 - (py / (TEX_H - 1)) * 180;
    for (let px = 0; px < TEX_W; px++) {
      const idx = py * TEX_W + px;
      const pxLeft = (px - 1 + TEX_W) % TEX_W;
      const pxRight = (px + 1) % TEX_W;
      const lonRaw = (px / (TEX_W - 1)) * 360 - 180;
      const lonNorm = lonRaw < 0 ? lonRaw + 360 : lonRaw;

      const h = base.heights[idx];
      const hLeft = base.heights[py * TEX_W + pxLeft];
      const hRight = base.heights[py * TEX_W + pxRight];
      const slope = base.slopes[idx];

      const grain = fbm(px * 0.08, py * 0.08, 3);
      const micro = valueNoise(px * 0.45 + 20, py * 0.45 + 5);

      let r = 175;
      let g = 66;
      let b = 28;

      if (layer === 'optical') {
        const warpX = valueNoise(px * 0.014, py * 0.014) * 26;
        const warpY = valueNoise(px * 0.014 + 31, py * 0.014 + 17) * 26;
        const mareNoise = fbm((px + warpX) * 0.02, (py + warpY) * 0.02, 3);

        const dSyrtis = angularDist(lat, lonNorm, 10, 68);
        const dAcidalia = angularDist(lat, lonNorm, 44, 338);
        const dCimmeria = angularDist(lat, lonNorm, -22, 150);
        const dErythraeum = angularDist(lat, lonNorm, -24, 326);
        const dMeridiani = angularDist(lat, lonNorm, -2, 355);
        const dUtopia = angularDist(lat, lonNorm, 46, 122);

        const mariaField =
          Math.exp(-(dSyrtis * dSyrtis) / 220) * 0.92 +
          Math.exp(-(dAcidalia * dAcidalia) / 340) * 0.84 +
          Math.exp(-(dCimmeria * dCimmeria) / 310) * 0.68 +
          Math.exp(-(dErythraeum * dErythraeum) / 290) * 0.72 +
          Math.exp(-(dMeridiani * dMeridiani) / 180) * 0.65 +
          Math.exp(-(dUtopia * dUtopia) / 260) * 0.42;

        const darkBasalt = Math.min(
          1.0,
          Math.max(0, (0.52 - mareNoise) * 2.35) * 0.68 + mariaField * 0.76
        );

        let ejectaGlow = 0;
        const rayCenters: [number, number][] = [
          [-24, 96],
          [8, 108],
          [-30, 260],
          [-18, 18],
        ];
        for (const [rLat, rLon] of rayCenters) {
          const d = angularDist(lat, lonNorm, rLat, rLon);
          if (d < 17) {
            const angle = Math.atan2(lat - rLat, ((lonNorm - rLon + 540) % 360) - 180);
            const rayStreak = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(angle * 11 + grain * 4);
            ejectaGlow += Math.exp(-(d * d) / 56) * (0.4 + 0.6 * rayStreak);
          }
        }
        ejectaGlow = Math.min(1, ejectaGlow);

        const ridgeLight = (hRight - hLeft) * 42;
        const baseR = 206 - darkBasalt * 140 + ejectaGlow * 40;
        const baseG = 78 - darkBasalt * 56 + ejectaGlow * 52;
        const baseB = 24 - darkBasalt * 18 + ejectaGlow * 28;

        r = baseR + h * 26 + ridgeLight + (grain - 0.5) * 26 + (micro - 0.5) * 20;
        g = baseG + h * 16 + ridgeLight * 0.68 + (grain - 0.5) * 14 + (micro - 0.5) * 11;
        b = baseB + h * 8 + ridgeLight * 0.35 + (micro - 0.5) * 8;

        if (Math.abs(lat) > 78) {
          const capBlend = Math.min(0.68, (Math.abs(lat) - 78) * 0.065 * (0.7 + grain * 0.6));
          r = r * (1 - capBlend) + 235 * capBlend;
          g = g * (1 - capBlend) + 224 * capBlend;
          b = b * (1 - capBlend) + 216 * capBlend;
        }
      } else if (layer === 'elevation') {
        const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (h + 0.85) / 1.85));
        if (t < 0.25) {
          const k = t / 0.25;
          r = 22 + k * 14;
          g = 48 + k * 120;
          b = 140 + k * 65;
        } else if (t < 0.5) {
          const k = (t - 0.25) / 0.25;
          r = 36 + k * 120;
          g = 168 + k * 25;
          b = 205 - k * 135;
        } else if (t < 0.78) {
          const k = (t - 0.5) / 0.28;
          r = 156 + k * 84;
          g = 193 - k * 115;
          b = 70 - k * 35;
        } else {
          const k = (t - 0.78) / 0.22;
          r = 240 + k * 15;
          g = 78 + k * 165;
          b = 35 + k * 210;
        }
        const shade = 0.85 + (hRight - hLeft) * 3.5;
        r *= shade;
        g *= shade;
        b *= shade;
        if (Math.abs(((t * 18) % 1) - 0.5) < 0.04) {
          r *= 0.8;
          g *= 0.8;
          b *= 0.8;
        }
      } else if (layer === 'slope') {
        if (slope < 0.28) {
          r = 26 + slope * 175;
          g = 150 + slope * 85;
          b = 96;
        } else if (slope < 0.55) {
          const k = (slope - 0.28) / 0.27;
          r = 124 + k * 125;
          g = 175 - k * 42;
          b = 42;
        } else {
          const k = (slope - 0.55) / 0.45;
          r = 238;
          g = 90 - k * 60;
          b = 42;
        }
      } else if (layer === 'atmosphere') {
        const pressureNorm = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 0.58 - h * 0.52));
        r = 28 + (1 - pressureNorm) * 145;
        g = 86 + pressureNorm * 135;
        b = 155 + pressureNorm * 95;
      } else {
        if (Math.abs(lat) > 77) {
          r = 205;
          g = 232;
          b = 242;
        } else if (h > 0.24) {
          r = 172;
          g = 68;
          b = 98;
        } else if (h < -0.24) {
          r = 52;
          g = 148;
          b = 166;
        } else {
          r = 202 - grain * 48;
          g = 140 - grain * 32;
          b = 72;
        }
      }

      const o = idx * 4;
      cImg.data[o] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(r)));
      cImg.data[o + 1] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(g)));
      cImg.data[o + 2] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(b)));
      cImg.data[o + 3] = 255;
    }
  }

  cCtx.putImageData(cImg, 0, 0);

  if (layer !== 'optical') {
    cCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)';
    cCtx.lineWidth = 1;
    for (let i = 1; i < 6; i++) {
      const y = (i / 6) * TEX_H;
      cCtx.beginPath();
      cCtx.moveTo(0, y);
      cCtx.lineTo(TEX_W, y);
      cCtx.stroke();
    }
    for (let j = 1; j < 12; j++) {
      const x = (j / 12) * TEX_W;
      cCtx.beginPath();
      cCtx.moveTo(x, 0);
      cCtx.lineTo(x, TEX_H);
      cCtx.stroke();
    }
  }

  const colorMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(colorCanvas);
  colorMap.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  colorMap.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  colorMap.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
  colorMap.anisotropy = maxAnisotropy;
  cachedLayerColorMaps.set(layer, colorMap);

  return {
    colorMap,
    bumpMap: base.bumpMap,
    normalMap: base.normalMap,
    roughnessMap: base.roughnessMap,
  };
}

/**
 * Generates and caches an astronomical deep-space Milky Way & star-dust sky dome texture.
 */
export function createDeepSpaceSkyTexture(): THREE.CanvasTexture {
  if (cachedSkyTex) return cachedSkyTex;

  const width = 768;
  const height = 384;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = width;
  canvas.height = height;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;

  ctx.fillStyle = '#010205';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  const img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, width, height);
  for (let py = 0; py < height; py++) {
    const v = py / height;
    for (let px = 0; px < width; px++) {
      const u = px / width;
      const galPlane = Math.sin(u * Math.PI * 2 + 0.6) * 0.16 + 0.48;
      const distPlane = Math.abs(v - galPlane);
      const band = Math.exp(-(distPlane * distPlane) / 0.011);

      const dust = fbm(u * 14, v * 7, 3);
      const rift = valueNoise(u * 26 + 9, v * 13 + 4);
      const lane = Math.max(0, band * (0.45 + dust * 0.9 - Math.max(0, rift - 0.55) * 1.4));

      const o = (py * width + px) * 4;
      img.data[o] = Math.min(255, Math.round(2 + lane * 28));
      img.data[o + 1] = Math.min(255, Math.round(4 + lane * 34));
      img.data[o + 2] = Math.min(255, Math.round(10 + lane * 56));
      img.data[o + 3] = 255;
    }
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);

  cachedSkyTex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  cachedSkyTex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  cachedSkyTex.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  cachedSkyTex.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
  return cachedSkyTex;
}

/**
 * Renders full territorial country-style region fills and borders directly onto a transparent
 * equirectangular CanvasTexture aligned 1:1 with the 3D Mars sphere.
 */
export function createTerritoryOverlayTexture(
  sites: MarsSite[],
  selectedSiteId: string,
  hoveredSiteId: string | null,
  maxAnisotropy: number
): THREE.CanvasTexture {
  const width = 1024;
  const height = 512;

  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = width;
  canvas.height = height;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

  const lonToUnwrappedX = (lon: number, refLon: number) => {
    const d = ((lon - refLon + 540) % 360) - 180;
    const unwrapped = refLon + d;
    const u = (unwrapped + 180) / 360;
    return u * width;
  };

  for (const site of sites) {
    const poly = site.boundaryPolygon;
    if (poly.length < 3) continue;

    const isSelected = site.id === selectedSiteId;
    const isHovered = site.id === hoveredSiteId;

    const rgb =
      site.safetyVerdict === 'SAFE'
        ? '16, 185, 129'
        : site.safetyVerdict === 'CAUTION'
          ? '245, 158, 11'
          : '239, 68, 68';

    const fillAlpha = isSelected ? 0.34 : isHovered ? 0.26 : 0.16;
    const strokeAlpha = isSelected ? 0.98 : isHovered ? 0.85 : 0.68;

    const refLon = poly[0][1];
    const pts = poly.map(([lat, lon]) => ({
      x: lonToUnwrappedX(lon, refLon),
      y: ((90 - lat) / 180) * height,
    }));

    for (const shift of [-width, 0, width]) {
      const minX = Math.min(...pts.map((p) => p.x + shift));
      const maxX = Math.max(...pts.map((p) => p.x + shift));
      if (maxX < 0 || minX > width) continue;

      ctx.beginPath();
      pts.forEach((p, idx) => {
        if (idx === 0) ctx.moveTo(p.x + shift, p.y);
        else ctx.lineTo(p.x + shift, p.y);
      });
      ctx.closePath();

      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${rgb}, ${fillAlpha})`;
      ctx.fill();

      ctx.lineWidth = isSelected ? 3.2 : 1.8;
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${rgb}, ${strokeAlpha})`;
      ctx.stroke();

      if (isSelected) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.setLineDash([7, 5]);
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85)';
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
      }
    }
  }

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  tex.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
  tex.anisotropy = maxAnisotropy;
  return tex;
}

import type { MarsSite } from '../types/mars';

let activeUtterance: SpeechSynthesisUtterance | null = null;
let narrateCallback: (() => void) | null = null;

export function narrateSite(site: MarsSite): void {
  if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;
  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  activeUtterance = null;

  const text = buildNarration(site);
  const utter = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
  utter.lang = 'en-US';
  utter.rate = 0.82;
  utter.pitch = 0.88;
  utter.volume = 1.0;

  // Pick a deep/authoritative voice if available
  const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
  const preferred =
    voices.find((v) => /Google UK English Male/i.test(v.name)) ??
    voices.find((v) => v.lang === 'en-US' && v.name.includes('Male')) ??
    voices.find((v) => v.lang === 'en-US') ??
    voices[0];
  if (preferred) utter.voice = preferred;

  utter.onend = () => {
    activeUtterance = null;
    narrateCallback?.();
    narrateCallback = null;
  };
  utter.onerror = () => {
    activeUtterance = null;
  };

  activeUtterance = utter;
  window.speechSynthesis.speak(utter);
}

export function stopNarration(): void {
  if ('speechSynthesis' in window) window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  activeUtterance = null;
}

export function isNarrating(): boolean {
  return 'speechSynthesis' in window && window.speechSynthesis.speaking;
}

export function onNarrationEnd(cb: () => void): void {
  narrateCallback = cb;
}

function buildNarration(site: MarsSite): string {
  const verdict =
    site.safetyVerdict === 'SAFE'
      ? 'This region is considered safe for human EVA operations, with manageable terrain and atmospheric conditions.'
      : site.safetyVerdict === 'CAUTION'
        ? 'Caution is advised. Terrain hazards and environmental factors require careful mission planning before human surface operations.'
        : 'This region is classified as hazardous. Robotic scouting is strongly recommended before any crewed EVA attempt.';

  const tempDesc =
    site.tempAvgC > -50
      ? 'relatively moderate'
      : site.tempAvgC > -70
        ? 'cold'
        : 'extremely cold';

  const o2Trace = Math.max(
    0.1,
    +(100 - site.atmosphereMix.co2 - site.atmosphereMix.n2 - site.atmosphereMix.ar).toFixed(2)
  );

  return [
    `Welcome to ${site.name}.`,
    `${site.geologySummary.slice(0, 240)}.`,
    `Atmospheric composition at this site:`,
    `Carbon dioxide: ${site.co2Percent} percent by volume.`,
    `Nitrogen: ${site.atmosphereMix.n2} percent.`,
    `Argon: ${site.atmosphereMix.ar} percent.`,
    `Trace gases including oxygen account for approximately ${o2Trace} percent.`,
    `Surface pressure is ${site.pressureKpa} kilopascals â€” roughly ${(site.pressureKpa / 101.325 * 100).toFixed(2)} percent of Earth sea-level pressure.`,
    `Thermal environment is ${tempDesc}, with an average surface temperature of ${site.tempAvgC} degrees Celsius.`,
    `Temperatures swing from a minimum of ${site.tempMinC} degrees to a maximum of ${site.tempMaxC} degrees Celsius.`,
    `Terrain elevation sits at ${site.elevationM.toLocaleString()} meters relative to the Martian datum.`,
    `Mean terrain slope is ${site.meanSlopeDeg} degrees, peaking at ${site.maxSlopeDeg} degrees in the most hazardous zones.`,
    `Daily radiation exposure is estimated at ${site.radiationMsvDay} millisieverts per day.`,
    `Dust opacity tau is ${site.dustOpacityTau}.`,
    `Subsurface water and ice potential: ${site.waterIcePotential}.`,
    `EVA assessment: ${verdict}`,
    site.evaRecommendation,
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(' ');
}

function getInitialMissionParams(): {
  view: 'landing' | 'console' | 'stories';
  mode: AppMode;
  siteId: string;
  chapterId: string;
  viewportMode: 'orbit' | 'panorama';
} {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return {
      view: 'landing',
      mode: 'archive',
      siteId: 'jezero',
      chapterId: 'jezero',
      viewportMode: 'orbit',
    };
  }
  const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  const siteParam = params.get('site');
  const modeParam = params.get('mode');
  const viewParam = params.get('view');
  const chapterParam = params.get('chapter');
  const validSite =
    siteParam && MARS_SITES.some((s) => s.id === siteParam) ? siteParam : 'jezero';
  const validMode: AppMode = modeParam === 'rover' ? 'rover' : 'archive';
  const validViewport: 'orbit' | 'panorama' = viewParam === 'panorama' ? 'panorama' : 'orbit';
  const validChapter = chapterParam || validSite;

  let initialView: 'landing' | 'console' | 'stories' = 'landing';
  if (viewParam === 'stories') {
    initialView = 'stories';
  } else if (siteParam || modeParam || viewParam === 'panorama' || viewParam === 'console') {
    initialView = 'console';
  }

  return {
    view: initialView,
    mode: validMode,
    siteId: validSite,
    chapterId: validChapter,
    viewportMode: validViewport,
  };
}

function playAnomalyTone() {
  try {
    const AudioCtx =
      window.AudioContext ||
      (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
    if (!AudioCtx) return;
    const ctx = new AudioCtx();
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'triangle';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(740, ctx.currentTime);
    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(520, ctx.currentTime + 0.16);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.18);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.19);
    setTimeout(() => void ctx.close(), 250);
  } catch {
    // Ignore autoplay restriction before user gesture
  }
}

export default function App() {
  const initial = useMemo(() => getInitialMissionParams(), []);

  const [view, setView] = useState<'landing' | 'console' | 'stories'>(initial.view);
  const [selectedChapterId, setSelectedChapterId] = useState<string>(initial.chapterId);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<AppMode>(initial.mode);
  const [viewportMode, setViewportMode] = useState<'orbit' | 'panorama'>(initial.viewportMode);
  const [selectedSiteId, setSelectedSiteId] = useState<string>(initial.siteId);
  const [activeLayer, setActiveLayer] = useState<DataLayerId>('optical');
  const [roverState, setRoverState] = useState<RoverTelemetryState>(INITIAL_ROVER_FALLBACK);
  const [dockOpen, setDockOpen] = useState<boolean>(() =>
    typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerWidth >= 768 : true
  );
  const [inspectorOpen, setInspectorOpen] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [hardwareModalOpen, setHardwareModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [qrModalOpen, setQrModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const prevAnomalyCountRef = useRef(0);

  const activeSite = useMemo(
    () => MARS_SITES.find((s) => s.id === selectedSiteId) ?? MARS_SITES[0],
    [selectedSiteId]
  );

  // Sync state attributes for clean inspection
  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.dataset.view = view;
    document.body.dataset.mode = mode;
    document.body.dataset.viewportMode = viewportMode;
    document.body.dataset.site = selectedSiteId;
    document.body.dataset.layer = activeLayer;
    document.body.dataset.roverPowered = String(roverState.powered);
  }, [view, mode, viewportMode, selectedSiteId, activeLayer, roverState.powered]);

  // Silently warm browser image cache for all 5 360Â° panoramas so switching is instantaneous
  useEffect(() => {
    if (view !== 'console') return;
    for (const site of MARS_SITES) {
      const preload = new Image();
      preload.src = site.panoramaImage;
    }
  }, [view]);

  // WebSocket real-time telemetry subscription + initial REST fetch
  useEffect(() => {
    let closed = false;
    let ws: WebSocket | null = null;
    let reconnectTimer = 0;

    fetch('/api/rover/state')
      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))
      .then((data: RoverTelemetryState | null) => {
        if (!closed && data && typeof data.x === 'number') {
          setRoverState(data);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});

    function connect() {
      if (closed) return;
      const proto = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
      ws = new WebSocket(`${proto}//${window.location.host}/ws`);
      ws.onmessage = (event) => {
        try {
          const msg = JSON.parse(event.data as string);
          if (msg && (msg.type === 'state:init' || msg.type === 'state:update')) {
            const nextState = msg.payload as RoverTelemetryState;
            setRoverState(nextState);
            if (
              nextState.anomalies.length > prevAnomalyCountRef.current &&
              nextState.powered
            ) {
              playAnomalyTone();
            }
            prevAnomalyCountRef.current = nextState.anomalies.length;
          }
        } catch {
          // Ignore malformed frame
        }
      };
      ws.onclose = () => {
        if (!closed) {
          reconnectTimer = window.setTimeout(connect, 1800);
        }
      };
    }

    connect();
    return () => {
      closed = true;
      clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
      ws?.close();
    };
  }, []);

  const sendControl = useCallback(async (payload: Record<string, unknown>) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/rover/control', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (json?.state) {
          setRoverState(json.state as RoverTelemetryState);
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Transient network error ignored; WS will resync
    }
  }, []);

  const handleSelectSite = useCallback(
    (siteId: string) => {
      setSelectedSiteId(siteId);
      setInspectorOpen(true);
      void sendControl({ action: 'site', siteId });
    },
    [sendControl]
  );

  const handleOpenPanorama = useCallback(
    (siteId: string) => {
      setSelectedSiteId(siteId);
      setViewportMode('panorama');
      setInspectorOpen(true);
      void sendControl({ action: 'site', siteId });
    },
    [sendControl]
  );

  const handleStartMission = useCallback(
    (siteId?: string) => {
      if (siteId) {
        setSelectedSiteId(siteId);
        void sendControl({ action: 'site', siteId });
      }
      setViewportMode('orbit');
      setView('console');
      setInspectorOpen(true);
    },
    [sendControl]
  );

  const handleTogglePower = useCallback(() => {
    const nextPowered = !roverState.powered;
    if (nextPowered) {
      setMode('rover');
      setInspectorOpen(true);
    }
    void sendControl({ action: 'power', powered: nextPowered, siteId: selectedSiteId });
  }, [roverState.powered, selectedSiteId, sendControl]);

  // WASD / Arrow keys steer rover in Live Rover Mode
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (view !== 'console' || mode !== 'rover' || !roverState.powered) return;
      const tag = (e.target as HTMLElement)?.tagName;
      if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA') return;

      const step = 4;
      if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'w' || e.key === 'W') {
        e.preventDefault();
        void sendControl({ action: 'move', dx: 0, dy: -step });
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S') {
        e.preventDefault();
        void sendControl({ action: 'move', dx: 0, dy: step });
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A') {
        e.preventDefault();
        void sendControl({ action: 'move', dx: -step, dy: 0 });
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd' || e.key === 'D') {
        e.preventDefault();
        void sendControl({ action: 'move', dx: step, dy: 0 });
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [view, mode, roverState.powered, sendControl]);

  return (
    <>
      {view === 'landing' ? (
        <MissionLanding
          sites={MARS_SITES}
          selectedSiteId={selectedSiteId}
          roverState={roverState}
          onStartMission={handleStartMission}
          onStartStories={(chapterId) => {
            if (chapterId) setSelectedChapterId(chapterId);
            setView('stories');
          }}
          onOpenQr={() => setQrModalOpen(true)}
        />
      ) : view === 'stories' ? (
        <MissionStoriesTheater
          allSites={MARS_SITES}
          roverState={roverState}
          initialChapterId={selectedChapterId}
          onExit={() => setView('console')}
          onSelectSiteInConsole={(siteId) => {
            setSelectedSiteId(siteId);
            setView('console');
          }}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="h-screen w-screen flex flex-col overflow-hidden bg-[#020306] text-slate-100">
          {/* Strict 3-Zone Top Bar Contract */}
          <header className="h-14 shrink-0 border-b border-white/10 bg-[#070A10] px-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-8">
            <a
              href="#home"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                setView('landing');
              }}
              className="font-display text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-50 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 hover:text-amber-300 transition-colors"
            >
              AresWalk
            </a>

            <nav
              aria-label="Mission navigation"
              className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-6 text-sm font-medium text-slate-400"
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setView('stories')}
                className="hover:text-amber-300 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-400 font-medium"
              >
                <Film className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Stories</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setMode('archive')}
                className={`hover:text-slate-100 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                  mode === 'archive' ? 'text-amber-300 underline underline-offset-4' : ''
                }`}
              >
                NASA Archive
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  setViewportMode((prev) => (prev === 'panorama' ? 'orbit' : 'panorama'))
                }
                className={`hover:text-slate-100 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                  viewportMode === 'panorama'
                    ? 'text-cyan-300 underline underline-offset-4'
                    : ''
                }`}
              >
                <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>{viewportMode === 'panorama' ? '3D Orbit' : '360Â° Surface'}</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setMode('rover');
                  setInspectorOpen(true);
                }}
                className={`hover:text-slate-100 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                  mode === 'rover' ? 'text-emerald-300 underline underline-offset-4' : ''
                }`}
              >
                Live Rover
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setQrModalOpen(true)}
                className="hover:text-slate-100 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5"
              >
                <QrCode className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                <span>Mission QR</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setHardwareModalOpen(true)}
                className="hover:text-slate-100 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                Hardware API
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setInspectorOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                className="hover:text-slate-100 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5"
              >
                {inspectorOpen ? (
                  <PanelRightClose className="w-4 h-4" />
                ) : (
                  <PanelRightOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
                )}
                <span>{inspectorOpen ? 'Hide Dossier' : 'Show Dossier'}</span>
              </button>
            </nav>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setQrModalOpen(true)}
                aria-label="Mission QR"
                className="md:hidden p-2 rounded border border-white/15 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-amber-400 transition-colors"
              >
                <QrCode className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleTogglePower}
                className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                  roverState.powered
                    ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 hover:bg-emerald-400'
                    : 'bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 hover:bg-amber-400'
                }`}
              >
                {roverState.powered ? 'Stop Rover' : 'Power On Rover'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </header>

          {/* Main Split Workspace: Unobstructed 3D Mars Stage + Docked Inspector */}
          <main className="flex-1 min-h-0 flex flex-col lg:flex-row overflow-hidden">
            {/* Left/Center 3D Mars Orbital or 360Â° Surface Panorama Viewport */}
            <section
              aria-label={
                viewportMode === 'panorama'
                  ? '360Â° Martian Surface Panorama'
                  : '3D Mars Orbital Viewport'
              }
              className="relative flex-1 min-h-[340px] overflow-hidden"
            >
              {viewportMode === 'panorama' ? (
                <MarsPanorama360
                  site={activeSite}
                  allSites={MARS_SITES}
                  onSelectSite={handleSelectSite}
                  onBackToGlobe={() => setViewportMode('orbit')}

              ) : (
                <>
                  <MarsGlobe3D
                    sites={MARS_SITES}
                    selectedSiteId={selectedSiteId}
                    activeLayer={activeLayer}
                    roverState={roverState}
                    onSelectSite={handleSelectSite}
                    onOpenPanorama={handleOpenPanorama}
                    showTerritories={true}
                  />

              {/* Top Viewport HUD Row: Orbital Readout + Instant Anomaly Alert (Zero Overlap) */}
              <div className="pointer-events-none absolute top-3 left-3 right-3 z-10 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between gap-2">

                  <div className="font-mono-tabular text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                    {LAYER_OPTIONS.find((l) => l.id === activeLayer)?.label} Â·{' '}
                    {activeSite.elevationM} m Â· {activeSite.meanSlopeDeg}Â°

                    Territory ~{Math.round(activeSite.areaKm2 / 1000)}k kmÂ² Â· Click region for 360Â°
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setViewportMode('panorama')}
                    className="pointer-events-auto inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xs border border-amber-400/60 bg-amber-500/15 hover:bg-amber-500 hover:text-slate-950 text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-200 transition-colors"

                              ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, #16308c, #24a8cd, #9cc146, #f04e23, #ffffff)'
                              : activeLayer === 'slope'
                                ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, #1c9862, #f5af2a, #ee3c2a)'
                                : activeLayer === 'atmosphere'
                                  ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, #ad569b, #1c86db, #1cd9f9)'
                                  : 'linear-gradient(90deg, #3494a6, #ca8c48, #ac4462)',
                        }}
                      />
                      <span>
                        {activeLayer === 'elevation'
                          ? '+21 km'
                          : activeLayer === 'slope'
                            ? '35Â°+ Risk'
                            : activeLayer === 'atmosphere'
                              ? 'Dense Basin'
                              : 'Volcanic'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {roverState.powered && roverState.anomalies.length > 0 && (
                  <div
                    role="status"
                    className="pointer-events-auto sm:max-w-sm border border-rose-500/80 bg-[#1B070C]/95 text-rose-100 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded shadow-lg flex items-start gap-2.5"
                  >
                    <AlertOctagon className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <div className="text-xs space-y-0.5 min-w-0">

                        {roverState.anomalies[0].title}
                      </div>
                      <div className="font-mono-tabular text-[11px] text-rose-300/90">
                        {roverState.anomalies[0].measuredValue} Â· {roverState.anomalies[0].nasaBaseline}
                      </div>
                    </div>

              </div>

              {/* Collapsible Bottom Control Dock (Zero Center Occlusion) */}
              <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-20 flex flex-col items-center w-[min(860px,95%)]">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setDockOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-t border border-b-0 border-white/15 bg-[#070A10]/95 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-300 hover:text-white whitespace-nowrap"

                            setMode('rover');

                          }}

                            mode === 'rover'
                              ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold'
                              : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-100'
                          }`}
                        >
                          2. Live Rover Mode
                        </button>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
                        {MARS_SITES.map((s) => {
                          const active = s.id === selectedSiteId;
                          return (
                            <button
                              key={s.id}
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => handleSelectSite(s.id)}
                              className={`px-2.5 py-1 text-xs rounded-xs border transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                                active
                                  ? 'border-amber-400 bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-200 font-semibold'
                                  : 'border-white/10 bg-[#030508] text-slate-300 hover:border-white/25'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {s.name.split(' Â· ')[0]}
                            </button>
                          );
                        })}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Row 2: Layered Orbital Data Overlay Controls + Quick 360 Trigger */}
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-1.5 border-t border-white/10">
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 whitespace-nowrap">
                        Orbital Layer:
                      </span>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
                        {LAYER_OPTIONS.map((layer) => {
                          const active = layer.id === activeLayer;
                          return (
                            <button
                              key={layer.id}
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => setActiveLayer(layer.id)}
                              className={`px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium rounded-xs border transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                                active
                                  ? 'border-cyan-400 bg-cyan-500/15 text-cyan-200'
                                  : 'border-white/10 bg-[#030508] text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {layer.label}
                            </button>
                          );
                        })}
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setViewportMode('panorama')}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold rounded-xs border border-amber-400/60 bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-200 hover:bg-amber-500 hover:text-slate-950 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap flex items-center gap-1"
                        >
                          <Globe className="w-3 h-3" />
                          <span>360Â° View</span>
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setSelectedChapterId(selectedSiteId);
                            setView('stories');
                          }}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold rounded-xs border border-violet-400/60 bg-violet-500/15 text-violet-200 hover:bg-violet-500 hover:text-slate-950 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap flex items-center gap-1"
                        >
                          <Film className="w-3 h-3" />
                          <span>Cinematic Story</span>
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
                </>
              )}
            </section>

            {/* Right Docked Mission Dossier / Live Rover Telemetry */}
            {inspectorOpen && (
              <aside
                aria-label="Mission Inspector"
                className="w-full lg:w-[420px] xl:w-[440px] h-[44vh] lg:h-full border-t lg:border-t-0 lg:border-l border-white/10 bg-[#080B11] shrink-0 overflow-hidden"
              >
                {mode === 'archive' ? (
                  <SiteDossierPanel
                    site={activeSite}
                    allSites={MARS_SITES}
                    activeLayer={activeLayer}
                    onSelectSite={handleSelectSite}
                    onOpenPanorama={() => setViewportMode('panorama')}
                    onSwitchToRover={() => {
                      setMode('rover');
                      if (!roverState.powered) {
                        void sendControl({
                          action: 'power',
                          powered: true,
                          siteId: selectedSiteId,
                        });
                      }
                    }}
                    onOpenQr={() => setQrModalOpen(true)}
                  />
                ) : (
                  <LiveRoverPanel
                    site={activeSite}
                    roverState={roverState}
                    onControl={sendControl}
                    onOpenHardwareModal={() => setHardwareModalOpen(true)}
                  />
                )}
              </aside>
            )}
          </main>
        </div>
      )}

      {hardwareModalOpen && (
        <HardwareApiModal
          activeSiteId={selectedSiteId}
          onClose={() => setHardwareModalOpen(false)}
          onOpenQr={() => setQrModalOpen(true)}
        />
      )}

      {qrModalOpen && (
        <QrShareModal
          activeSite={activeSite}
          onClose={() => setQrModalOpen(false)}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

import express from 'express';
import http from 'node:http';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { WebSocketServer, WebSocket } from 'ws';
import { GoogleGenAI } from '@google/genai';
import { MARS_SITES } from './src/data/marsSites.ts';
import { evaluateRoverAnomalies } from './src/utils/marsLogic.ts';
import type { RoverTelemetryState } from './src/types/mars.ts';

const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const PORT = 3000;
const ROCK_CAM_FALLBACK = '/assets/mars/esp32_rock_obstacle_1794654803410.jpg';

let aiClient: GoogleGenAI | null = null;
function getGenAI(): GoogleGenAI | null {
  if (aiClient) return aiClient;
  const key = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY;
  if (!key) return null;
  aiClient = new GoogleGenAI({
    apiKey: key,
    httpOptions: {
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'aistudio-build',
      },
    },
  });
  return aiClient;
}

const voiceCache = new Map<string, string>();

function getSite(siteId: string) {
  return MARS_SITES.find((s) => s.id === siteId) ?? MARS_SITES[0];
}

function createInitialState(siteId = 'jezero'): RoverTelemetryState {
  const site = getSite(siteId);
  const startWp = site.waypoints[0] ?? { x: 20, y: 78 };
  return {
    powered: false,
    activeSiteId: site.id,
    patrolActive: false,
    x: startWp.x,
    y: startWp.y,
    headingDeg: 45,
    distanceCm: 145,
    co2Ppm: site.co2BaselinePpm,
    pitchDeg: site.meanSlopeDeg,
    rollDeg: 1.2,
    batteryPct: 96,
    lastUpdated: Date.now(),
    source: 'standby',
    latestCameraFrame: null,
    cameraFrameTimestamp: null,
    cameraLabel: 'ESP32-CAM Standby',
    anomalies: [],
    trail: [{ x: startWp.x, y: startWp.y, hasAnomaly: false, timestamp: Date.now() }],
  };
}

let roverState: RoverTelemetryState = createInitialState('jezero');
let patrolPhase = 0;

function recomputeAndRecord(nextSource: RoverTelemetryState['source']) {
  const site = getSite(roverState.activeSiteId);
  const now = Date.now();
  roverState.source = nextSource;
  roverState.lastUpdated = now;
  roverState.anomalies = evaluateRoverAnomalies(
    {
      distanceCm: roverState.distanceCm,
      co2Ppm: roverState.co2Ppm,
      pitchDeg: roverState.pitchDeg,
      rollDeg: roverState.rollDeg,
    },
    site,
    now
  );

  if (roverState.powered) {
    const lastPt = roverState.trail[roverState.trail.length - 1];
    const moved =
      !lastPt || Math.hypot(roverState.x - lastPt.x, roverState.y - lastPt.y) >= 0.8;
    const hasAnomaly = roverState.anomalies.length > 0;
    if (moved || (lastPt && lastPt.hasAnomaly !== hasAnomaly)) {
      roverState.trail = [
        ...roverState.trail.slice(-119),
        { x: roverState.x, y: roverState.y, hasAnomaly, timestamp: now },
      ];
    }
  }
}

async function startServer() {
  const app = express();
  app.use(express.json({ limit: '5mb' }));
  app.use('/api/camera-frame', express.raw({ type: 'image/jpeg', limit: '5mb' }));

  const server = http.createServer(app);
  const wss = new WebSocketServer({ server, path: '/ws' });

  function broadcastState() {
    const payload = JSON.stringify({ type: 'state:update', payload: roverState });
    for (const client of wss.clients) {
      if (client.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        client.send(payload);
      }
    }
  }

  wss.on('connection', (ws) => {
    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'state:init', payload: roverState }));
  });

  // Autonomous obstacle-avoidance patrol loop when powered & patrolActive
  setInterval(() => {
    if (!roverState.powered || !roverState.patrolActive) return;
    const site = getSite(roverState.activeSiteId);
    const wps = site.waypoints;
    if (wps.length < 2) return;

    // Pause forward motion if critical obstacle detected
    if (roverState.distanceCm <= 25) return;

    patrolPhase = (patrolPhase + 0.035) % wps.length;
    const idxA = Math.floor(patrolPhase) % wps.length;
    const idxB = (idxA + 1) % wps.length;
    const t = patrolPhase - Math.floor(patrolPhase);

    const nextX = wps[idxA].x + (wps[idxB].x - wps[idxA].x) * t;
    const nextY = wps[idxA].y + (wps[idxB].y - wps[idxA].y) * t;
    const dx = nextX - roverState.x;
    const dy = nextY - roverState.y;
    if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 0.05) {
      roverState.headingDeg = (Math.atan2(dx, -dy) * 180) / Math.PI;
    }

    roverState.x = Math.round(nextX * 10) / 10;
    roverState.y = Math.round(nextY * 10) / 10;
    roverState.batteryPct = Math.max(12, Math.round((roverState.batteryPct - 0.02) * 10) / 10);

    recomputeAndRecord(roverState.source === 'hardware' ? 'hardware' : 'bench-test');
    broadcastState();
  }, 550);

  app.get('/api/health', (_req, res) => {
    res.json({ ok: true, clients: wss.clients.size, powered: roverState.powered });
  });

  app.get('/api/sites', (_req, res) => {
    res.json({ sites: MARS_SITES });
  });

  app.get('/api/rover/state', (_req, res) => {
    res.json(roverState);
  });

  // Physical ESP32 / Arduino Wi-Fi telemetry ingestion endpoint
  app.post('/api/telemetry', (req, res) => {
    const body = req.body ?? {};
    roverState.powered = true;
    if (typeof body.siteId === 'string') {
      roverState.activeSiteId = getSite(body.siteId).id;
    }
    if (typeof body.distanceCm === 'number' && Number.isFinite(body.distanceCm)) {
      roverState.distanceCm = Math.max(1, Math.min(400, body.distanceCm));
    }
    if (typeof body.co2Ppm === 'number' && Number.isFinite(body.co2Ppm)) {
      roverState.co2Ppm = Math.max(0, Math.min(10000, body.co2Ppm));
    }
    if (typeof body.pitchDeg === 'number' && Number.isFinite(body.pitchDeg)) {
      roverState.pitchDeg = Math.max(-90, Math.min(90, body.pitchDeg));
    }
    if (typeof body.rollDeg === 'number' && Number.isFinite(body.rollDeg)) {
      roverState.rollDeg = Math.max(-90, Math.min(90, body.rollDeg));
    }
    if (typeof body.x === 'number' && Number.isFinite(body.x)) {
      roverState.x = Math.max(2, Math.min(98, body.x));
    }
    if (typeof body.y === 'number' && Number.isFinite(body.y)) {
      roverState.y = Math.max(2, Math.min(98, body.y));
    }
    if (typeof body.headingDeg === 'number' && Number.isFinite(body.headingDeg)) {
      roverState.headingDeg = body.headingDeg;
    }
    if (typeof body.batteryPct === 'number' && Number.isFinite(body.batteryPct)) {
      roverState.batteryPct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, body.batteryPct));
    }

    recomputeAndRecord('hardware');
    broadcastState();
    res.json({ ok: true, anomalies: roverState.anomalies, state: roverState });
  });

  // Physical ESP32-CAM snapshot endpoint (JSON base64 or raw image/jpeg)
  app.post('/api/camera-frame', (req, res) => {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (Buffer.isBuffer(req.body)) {
      const b64 = req.body.toString('base64');
      roverState.latestCameraFrame = `data:image/jpeg;base64,${b64}`;
      roverState.cameraFrameTimestamp = now;
      roverState.cameraLabel = 'ESP32-CAM Live Frame';
    } else if (req.body && typeof req.body.imageBase64 === 'string') {
      const raw = req.body.imageBase64;
      roverState.latestCameraFrame = raw.startsWith('data:')
        ? raw
        : `data:image/jpeg;base64,${raw}`;
      roverState.cameraFrameTimestamp = now;
      roverState.cameraLabel =
        typeof req.body.label === 'string' ? req.body.label : 'ESP32-CAM Live Frame';
    } else {
      res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Provide imageBase64 string or image/jpeg binary' });
      return;
    }

    roverState.powered = true;
    roverState.lastUpdated = now;
    broadcastState();
    res.json({ ok: true, timestamp: now });
  });

  // Bilingual Gemini TTS narration endpoint with in-memory cache
  app.post('/api/stories/voiceover', async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { text, language = 'en' } = req.body ?? {};
      if (!text || typeof text !== 'string') {
        res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Text is required' });
        return;
      }

      const cacheKey = `${language}:${text.trim()}`;
      if (voiceCache.has(cacheKey)) {
        res.json({ ok: true, audio: voiceCache.get(cacheKey), cached: true });
        return;
      }

      const ai = getGenAI();
      if (!ai) {
        res.json({
          ok: false,
          fallback: 'speech-synthesis',
          error: 'No GEMINI_API_KEY configured',
        });
        return;
      }

      const isArabic = language === 'ar';
      const voiceName = isArabic ? 'Kore' : 'Puck';
      const style = isArabic
        ? 'Clear, authoritative Arabic planetary documentary narrator'
        : 'Clear, authoritative planetary mission documentary commander';

      const modelName = process.env.GEMINI_TTS_MODEL || 'gemini-2.5-flash';

      let base64Audio: string | undefined;
      let mimeType = 'audio/wav';

      try {
        const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
          model: modelName,
          contents: [
            {
              role: 'user',
              parts: [
                {
                  text: text.trim(),
                  speechMetadata: {
                    style,
                  },
                },
              ],
            },
          ],
          config: {
            responseModalities: ['AUDIO'],
            speechConfig: {
              voiceConfig: {
                prebuiltVoiceConfig: { voiceName },
              },
            },
          },
        });

        const part = response.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0];
        base64Audio = part?.inlineData?.data;
        if (part?.inlineData?.mimeType) {
          mimeType = part.inlineData.mimeType;
        }
      } catch (primaryErr) {
        // Fallback to gemini-2.0-flash if primary model fails
        if (modelName !== 'gemini-2.0-flash') {
          try {
            const fallbackRes = await ai.models.generateContent({
              model: 'gemini-2.0-flash',
              contents: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ text: text.trim() }] }],
              config: {
                responseModalities: ['AUDIO'],
                speechConfig: {
                  voiceConfig: {
                    prebuiltVoiceConfig: { voiceName },
                  },
                },
              },
            });
            const part = fallbackRes.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0];
            base64Audio = part?.inlineData?.data;
            if (part?.inlineData?.mimeType) {
              mimeType = part.inlineData.mimeType;
            }
          } catch {
            throw primaryErr;
          }
        } else {
          throw primaryErr;
        }
      }

      if (base64Audio) {
        const dataUri = `data:${mimeType};base64,${base64Audio}`;
        voiceCache.set(cacheKey, dataUri);
        res.json({ ok: true, audio: dataUri });
      } else {
        res.json({
          ok: false,
          fallback: 'speech-synthesis',
          error: 'No audio part returned',
        });
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      res.json({ ok: false, fallback: 'speech-synthesis', error: message });
    }
  });

  // UI Control & Hardware Bench Test actions
  app.post('/api/rover/control', (req, res) => {
    const body = req.body ?? {};
    const action = body.action;
    const site = getSite(
      typeof body.siteId === 'string' ? body.siteId : roverState.activeSiteId
    );

    if (action === 'power') {
      const nextPowered = Boolean(body.powered);
      roverState.powered = nextPowered;
      if (nextPowered) {
        roverState.patrolActive = true;
        roverState.source = 'bench-test';
        roverState.latestCameraFrame = site.surfaceImage;
        roverState.cameraLabel = `Rover NavCam Â· ${site.name}`;
        roverState.cameraFrameTimestamp = Date.now();
      } else {
        roverState.patrolActive = false;
        roverState.source = 'standby';
      }
    } else if (action === 'site') {
      const wasPowered = roverState.powered;
      const wasPatrol = roverState.patrolActive;
      roverState = createInitialState(site.id);
      patrolPhase = 0;
      roverState.powered = wasPowered;
      roverState.patrolActive = wasPowered && wasPatrol;
      if (wasPowered) {
        roverState.source = 'bench-test';
        roverState.latestCameraFrame = site.surfaceImage;
        roverState.cameraLabel = `Rover NavCam Â· ${site.name}`;
        roverState.cameraFrameTimestamp = Date.now();
      }
    } else if (action === 'patrol') {
      if (!roverState.powered) roverState.powered = true;
      roverState.patrolActive = Boolean(body.patrolActive);
    } else if (action === 'move') {
      if (!roverState.powered) roverState.powered = true;
      roverState.patrolActive = false;
      const dx = Number(body.dx) || 0;
      const dy = Number(body.dy) || 0;
      roverState.x = Math.max(4, Math.min(96, Math.round((roverState.x + dx) * 10) / 10));
      roverState.y = Math.max(4, Math.min(96, Math.round((roverState.y + dy) * 10) / 10));
      if (dx !== 0 || dy !== 0) {
        roverState.headingDeg = (Math.atan2(dx, -dy) * 180) / Math.PI;
      }
    } else if (action === 'reset') {
      const keepPowered = roverState.powered;
      roverState = createInitialState(site.id);
      patrolPhase = 0;
      roverState.powered = keepPowered;
      roverState.patrolActive = keepPowered;
      if (keepPowered) {
        roverState.latestCameraFrame = site.surfaceImage;
        roverState.cameraLabel = `Rover NavCam Â· ${site.name}`;
        roverState.cameraFrameTimestamp = Date.now();
      }
    } else if (action === 'bench') {
      roverState.powered = true;
      const scenario = body.scenario;
      if (scenario === 'rock') {
        roverState.distanceCm = 16.4;
        roverState.latestCameraFrame = ROCK_CAM_FALLBACK;
        roverState.cameraFrameTimestamp = Date.now();
        roverState.cameraLabel = 'ESP32-CAM Â· Unmapped Rock Obstacle (16.4 cm)';
      } else if (scenario === 'tilt') {
        roverState.pitchDeg = 24.5;
        roverState.rollDeg = 16.2;
      } else if (scenario === 'co2') {
        roverState.co2Ppm = 2480;
      } else if (scenario === 'nominal') {
        roverState.distanceCm = 148;
        roverState.co2Ppm = site.co2BaselinePpm;
        roverState.pitchDeg = site.meanSlopeDeg;
        roverState.rollDeg = 1.2;
        roverState.latestCameraFrame = site.surfaceImage;
        roverState.cameraFrameTimestamp = Date.now();
        roverState.cameraLabel = `Rover NavCam Â· ${site.name} (Clear)`;
      }
    } else {
      res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Unknown control action' });
      return;
    }

    recomputeAndRecord(roverState.powered ? 'bench-test' : 'standby');
    broadcastState();
    res.json({ ok: true, state: roverState });
  });

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
    const { createServer: createViteServer } = await import('vite');
    const vite = await createViteServer({
      server: { middlewareMode: true },
      appType: 'spa',
    });
    app.use(vite.middlewares);
  } else {
    const distDir = path.join(__dirname, 'dist');
    app.use(express.static(distDir));
    app.get('*', (_req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(distDir, 'index.html'));
    });
  }

  server.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', () => {
    console.log(`AresWalk Mission Server listening on http://localhost:${PORT}`);
  });
}

startServer().catch((err) => {
  console.error('Failed to start server:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});

import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
    resolve: {
      dedupe: ['react', 'react-dom'],
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modifyâ€”file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});
